## [BUG-013] `docs/dev-environment.md` mischaracterizes `make dev-py`'s cwd, silently breaking the default `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `Makefile:35-38` (`dev-py` target), `apps/ai-server/src/config.py` (`prompts_base_dir` field + `resolve_prompts_dir()`), documentation claim at `docs/dev-environment.md:147`
**Linked:** PLAN-2026-W28-D + AMENDMENT (dev-environment.md fact-verification gate), §1.2/§5.1(b) of the same doc (which independently and correctly states the general risk this bug instantiates)
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | Read `Makefile:35-38` — the `dev-py` target's ai-server line is `cd apps/ai-server && uv run uvicorn src.main:app --reload --port 8001` |
| 2 | Read `docs/dev-environment.md:147` — it describes this exact command, citing `Makefile:35-38`, as running "레포 루트에서" (from the repo root) |
| 3 | Read `apps/ai-server/src/config.py:71-74,96-98` — `prompts_base_dir` defaults to the relative path `"docs/ai/prompts"`, resolved via `Path(self.prompts_base_dir)` with no project-root anchoring (unlike `f1.py:1341-1342`/`f2.py:473-474`/`safety_matrix.py:270-271`, which explicitly correct `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` to `PROJECT_ROOT`-absolute when unset — a fact `docs/dev-environment.md:163` itself documents) |
| 4 | `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_013.py -v` |

**Expected:** Per `docs/dev-environment.md:147`'s own claim, `make dev-py` runs `uv run uvicorn src.main:app --reload --port 8001` "from the repo root" — in which case the default relative `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` (`docs/ai/prompts`) would resolve correctly against the real prompts directory at the repo root.
**Actual:** `Makefile:38` explicitly `cd apps/ai-server &&`s before invoking uvicorn, so the FastAPI process's cwd is `apps/ai-server`, not the repo root. `Path("docs/ai/prompts")` resolved from that cwd is `apps/ai-server/docs/ai/prompts`, which does not exist (verified: `test -d apps/ai-server/docs/ai/prompts` → absent; `test -d docs/ai/prompts` → present). `PromptLoader.load_system_prompt()` (`src/prompts/loader.py:25-42`) fails lazily with `FileNotFoundError` only when a prompt-dependent endpoint (e.g. `/ai/chat/respond`) is actually hit — the server starts and `/health` responds normally, masking the defect until request time. A developer following `docs/dev-environment.md`'s own advice — run `make dev-py` (§5.1a, described as the "safer" option) and leave `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` unset (§5.1b: "대부분 비워둬도 된다") — lands in this broken state with no warning.

**Root cause:** Two independent gaps compound: (1) `Makefile`'s `dev-py` target changes directory per-service before launching uvicorn, so cwd ≠ repo root for the ai-server process; (2) `config.py`'s `prompts_base_dir` default is a bare relative path with no `PROJECT_ROOT` anchoring in `resolve_prompts_dir()`, unlike the three validation scripts (`f1.py`/`f2.py`/`safety_matrix.py`) that already correct for exactly this class of cwd mismatch. `docs/dev-environment.md` correctly documents gap (2) in the abstract (§5.1b) but mischaracterizes `make dev-py` itself (§5.1a) as unaffected by it, when it is the primary, doc-recommended trigger for the gap.

**Minimal repro:** `apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_013.py` — `test_makefile_dev_py_still_cds_into_ai_server_before_uvicorn` (PASS, confirms the precondition), `test_default_prompts_base_dir_does_not_exist_from_ai_server_cwd` (PASS, reproduces the defect), `test_default_prompts_base_dir_exists_from_repo_root_cwd` (PASS, contrast case isolating the defect to cwd), `test_project_root_sanity` (PASS, guards the shared `PROJECT_ROOT` fixture).

**Proposed fix (not prescribed):** either (a) code fix — anchor `config.py`'s `resolve_prompts_dir()` to a `PROJECT_ROOT`-relative path when the value is unset/relative, mirroring `f1.py`/`f2.py`/`safety_matrix.py`'s existing correction (most robust — fixes the container path too, not just `make dev-py`); or (b) Makefile fix — change `dev-py`'s ai-server line to preserve repo-root cwd (e.g. `uv run --directory apps/ai-server uvicorn src.main:app ...` if supported, or set `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` inline in the Makefile recipe); or (c) doc-only fix (weakest — leaves the underlying trap live for anyone invoking `uv run uvicorn` by hand) — correct `docs/dev-environment.md:147`'s "레포 루트에서" claim and add an explicit warning that `make dev-py` requires `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR` to be set to an absolute path.

### Status update | 2026-07-08 | qa (re-gate after writer's doc fix)

**Status:** doc dimension (fix option c) resolved; code dimension (options a/b) remains open — not attempted, no EXP invalidation implied
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-08

**1. Doc fix verified against source.** `docs/dev-environment.md` §5.1(a) (~line 147) now quotes `Makefile:38` verbatim ("`cd apps/ai-server && uv run uvicorn src.main:app --reload --port 8001`이다. **레포 루트에서가 아니라 `apps/ai-server`로 cd한 뒤 uvicorn이 뜬다** (BUG-013 — 이 문서가 과거 "레포 루트에서 실행"이라고 서술했던 것은 오기였다)") — matches `Makefile:38` byte-for-byte, cites BUG-013. §5.1(b) rewritten: states plainly that only the CLI validation scripts (`f1.py`/`f2.py`/`safety_matrix.py`) auto-correct `PROMPTS_BASE_DIR`, the uvicorn server does not, and instructs `.env` to carry a repo-root-absolute path. Re-verified the three CLI auto-correction citations directly: `f1.py:1341-1342`, `f2.py:473-474`, `safety_matrix.py:270-271` all read `if not os.environ.get("PROMPTS_BASE_DIR"): os.environ["PROMPTS_BASE_DIR"] = str(PROJECT_ROOT / "docs" / "ai" / "prompts")` — citations accurate, no new unverified claim introduced. `config.py:71-74` (`prompts_base_dir` default `"docs/ai/prompts"`) and `:96-98` (`resolve_prompts_dir()` — bare `Path(self.prompts_base_dir)`, no anchoring) confirmed to match the doc's description exactly. §5.4 troubleshooting row ("프롬프트 엔드포인트에서 `FileNotFoundError`" → cwd cause → `.env` absolute-path fix, cites BUG-013) is consistent with both. No contradiction with §1.2's own auto-correction note (§1.2 only covers DB `DATABASE_URL`/Docker-mount scope, not this specific claim). **Doc dimension of BUG-013 closes as resolved.**

**2. Empirical `uv run` cwd test (this gate, live shell, repo root):**
```
$ uv run --directory apps/ai-server python -c "import os; print(os.getcwd())"
/home/neuroai/users/dhkim/aichampion/neurosync/apps/ai-server

$ uv run --project apps/ai-server python -c "import os; print(os.getcwd())"
/home/neuroai/users/dhkim/aichampion/neurosync
```
Cross-checked against `uv run --help` (uv 0.10.9): `--directory <DIRECTORY>` = "Change to the given directory prior to running the command" (i.e. chdirs, confirmed above); `--project <PROJECT>` = "Discover a project in the given directory" (does not chdir the running process, confirmed above — cwd stays at repo root).

**Finding: BUG-013's own "proposed fix (b)" text above, and `apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_013.py::test_makefile_dev_py_still_cds_into_ai_server_before_uvicorn`'s docstring (pre-fix), are both factually wrong.** They cite `uv run --directory apps/ai-server uvicorn ...` as an example that "preserve[s] repo-root cwd" — the empirical test above shows the opposite: `--directory` chdirs into `apps/ai-server`, identical in effect to the existing `cd apps/ai-server &&`. The flag that actually preserves repo-root cwd while still resolving `apps/ai-server`'s environment/pyproject is `--project`, not `--directory`.
- **Proposed fix (b), corrected:** if the Makefile-fix route is taken, the ai-server line should read `uv run --project apps/ai-server uvicorn src.main:app --reload --port 8001` (run from repo root, no `cd`), not `--directory`. This correction is appended here per this project's append-only convention; the original (b) text above is left unedited as historical record — do not follow it literally.
- **`test_bug_013.py` docstring:** confirmed factually wrong in the same way (cited `--directory` as the cwd-preserving example). This is squarely a docstring inaccuracy, not a test-logic defect — the test's assertions (`test_makefile_dev_py_still_cds_into_ai_server_before_uvicorn` etc.) do not exercise `uv run` flags at all and remain correct. Per this task's docstring-only-edit allowance, qa corrected the docstring in place (test logic untouched, replaced `--directory` example with `--project`, cited this empirical result and `uv run --help`) — re-ran `tests/repro/test_bug_013.py` and the full suite after the edit: **627 passed, 0 failed** (identical count, confirming no behavior change).

**3. Full suite:** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **627 passed, 0 failed**, both before and after the docstring edit.

**4. Diff-set check:** `git status` shows `docs/dev-environment.md` (new, untracked — writer's fix, as expected) plus `apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_013.py` (untracked, pre-existing from BUG-013's original filing, now with the docstring correction above) and `apps/ai-server/.env.example` (untracked, filemanager's PLAN-2026-W28-D step 1b). Six additional tracked files show modifications — `Makefile`, `README.md`, `docs/prd/PRD_neuro-sync.md`, `docs/todo_plan/PLAN_neuro-sync.md`, `infra/README.md`, `infra/deploy/docker-compose.yml` — all Redis-removal edits (compose service block, `REDIS_URL`, `depends_on:redis`, topology-diagram lines, tree-listing comments). These match PLAN-2026-W28-D's AMENDMENT (2026-07-08, "redis 제거") item (1)'s filemanager scope description line-for-line and are unrelated to the BUG-013 doc fix — a parallel, separately-authorized work stream (step 1d), not a stray change bleeding out of writer's edit scope. `grep -rniI redis` across the repo (md/py/Makefile/yml/yaml/ts/tsx, excluding node_modules/.venv/lockfiles) returns zero hits, and `docs/dev-environment.md` itself contains no Redis reference — consistent with the AMENDMENT's redis-exclusion requirement for the doc. No unexplained file changes found.

**GATE: PASS** — BUG-013 doc dimension closes as resolved (writer's fix verified accurate, no contradictions, no new unverified claims); the `--directory`/`--project` factual question is settled empirically and corrected in both `test_bug_013.py`'s docstring (fixed here) and this entry's own proposed-fix-(b) text (annotated above, original left intact per append-only convention); full suite green at 627; diff set accounted for. BUG-013's code dimension (options a/c — anchoring `resolve_prompts_dir()` to `PROJECT_ROOT`, or a corrected Makefile fix using `--project`) remains **open** for developer; no EXP-NNN uses this code path, so no invalidation applies.

## [VAL-009] F2 v2's risk lexicon misses the burdensomeness/meaninglessness paraphrase families of this project's own established taxonomy | 2026-07-08 | critic

**Target:** `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py` (`_RISK_PHRASES`, lines 76-92)
**Issue:** ADR-014/VAL-006/REV-008's remediation added a code-level risk-lexicon filter that mechanically rejects evidence quotes containing 15 phrases (passive-SI family, "자해", "손목", "목숨", "유서"). Replaying the actual `docs/ai/simulation_results/VP-003/VP-003_20260708_120459_domain_inference.json` (run1) evidence set — the same artifact REV-008 adjudicated — against this lexicon: of REV-008's own "~9-10 of 13" broader-taxonomy-flagged quotes, the filter correctly rejects 6 (the "살고 싶지 않"/"사라지고 싶" family) but does not match 4: turn_5/turn_9 ("...짐이 되기 싫어서." — burdensomeness, worded differently from the lexicon's literal "내가 없으면") and turn_10/turn_12 ("...살아서 뭐하나." — meaninglessness, no lexicon entry for "사는 게 의미가 없다" or any paraphrase of it). These 4 quotes ship as accepted, post-cascade evidence for the `depression` domain candidate under the current code — i.e., replaying the exact scenario REV-008 ruled blocking through the shipped "fix" still produces a candidate citing burden/meaninglessness ideation as domain evidence. Run2 (3/3 quotes, all in the strict passive-SI family) is fully caught by contrast, showing the gap is specific to the two paraphrase families, not a general filter failure. The shipped regression fixture (`tests/test_f2_grounding.py::TestRiskLexiconFilter`) tests only the 2 quotes REV-008 quoted verbatim (run1 turn_0, run2 turn_1) — no fixture exercises run1's other 11 quotes or either paraphrase family.
**Why blocking:** Not filed as blocking. The literal violation REV-008 ruled on (the 2 quotes it quoted verbatim) is now closed, deterministically and verifiably — genuine, non-cosmetic progress. It is major because DR-007/any future report must not describe this as closing REV-008's own broader-taxonomy finding (a false "recurrence = 0" claim would repeat the exact claim-vs-evidence gap this project has repeatedly had to file VAL entries for — VAL-002, VAL-004, VAL-006), and because the residual gap is now demonstrated on the exact artifact/persona (VP-003) DATASET-004 already flags for extra scrutiny, not a theoretical edge case.
**Linked review:** REV-009
**Status:** open

### Recommended resolution (for developer, not prescribed here)

Add "짐이 되"/"부담이 되" and "살아서 뭐하"/"사는 게 의미" (space-insensitive, matching the existing `_contains_any` convention) to `_RISK_PHRASES`. If deferred past EXP-005, REV-009's pre-registered secondary metric (manual broader-taxonomy audit of all accepted evidence quotes, all VPs, both arms) is the mandatory backstop, and DR-007 must state the residual gap and its measured size explicitly rather than claim closure.

## [VAL-010] RAG-arm activation precondition from REV-007 finding #3 (`risk_assessment`-as-Stage-1-query) remains unmet at the point the RAG arm first activates | 2026-07-08 | critic

**Target:** `apps/ai-server/src/f2.py:58` (`_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS`)
**Issue:** REV-007 (finding #3, `discussion.md`) identified that F2's Stage 1 embeds the `risk_assessment` F1 slot (composed from the Safety Probe/SI-screen exchange — genuinely risk-worded narration, `apps/ai-server/src/f1.py:360-373`) directly as a retrieval query, which systematically biases which `case_card`/`qa` chunks Stage 1 returns toward risk/crisis-topic content for the higher-risk personas in this project's own batch (VP-003/VP-004) — a subtler channel for risk content to shape domain evidence than direct quote-citation, since a resulting chunk could be legitimately grounded (passes the whitelist) while its retrievability was driven purely by risk-language similarity. REV-007 ruled this "must be addressed (dedicated analysis or mitigation, e.g. excluding `risk_assessment` from query text, or auditing `retrieval_meta.queries` for risk-adjacent content post-hoc) before the RAG arm is ever activated" and noted it was "currently dormant" only because the RAG arm was DB-blocked at the time. `_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS` (`f2.py:58`) is unchanged since that finding — `risk_assessment` remains one of 3 query source slots — and no query-content audit step appears in the PLAN-2026-W28-E EXP-005 protocol text. The RAG arm is about to activate for the first time this mission (DB now live per HANDOFF), so this precondition is no longer dormant.
**Why blocking:** Not filed as blocking. The mitigation REV-007 itself offered (a post-hoc audit of `retrieval_meta.queries`, already captured in the artifact) is procedurally cheap and requires no code change, so this is resolvable as part of executing EXP-005 rather than requiring a delay. It is major, not minor, because it is a previously-ruled, explicitly-stated precondition for RAG-arm activation that was not satisfied before the RAG arm's actual first activation, and because an unaudited retrieval-bias channel could silently affect which evidence RAG-arm runs even cite, undermining the fabrication-0/top-1/top-3 results' interpretability for exactly the personas DATASET-004 flags for extra scrutiny.
**Linked review:** REV-009
**Status:** open

### Recommended resolution (for experiment-tracker, not prescribed here)

Add the `retrieval_meta.queries` risk-content audit (manual review of every RAG-mode VP-003/VP-004 run's `queries` field for risk-adjacent phrasing, cross-referenced against the same broader taxonomy REV-009/VAL-009 use) as an explicit EXP-005 execution step, before RAG-arm fabrication/latency/scoring results are reported as clean. Longer-term (not a precondition for this run): consider excluding `risk_assessment` from Stage-1 query composition in code, per REV-007's other named option.

### Status update | 2026-07-08 | critic (REV-010)

**Status:** open — narrowed and escalated with new evidence, not closed

EXP-005 satisfied this entry's stated precondition for the imminent batch: the post-hoc `retrieval_meta.queries` audit was executed and reported uncensored (`result.md` EXP-005, "VAL-010 finding" section) — that specific obligation is discharged. This entry does not close, because: (1) the underlying code (`apps/ai-server/src/f2.py:58`, `_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS`) is unchanged — `risk_assessment` still feeds Stage-1 retrieval queries verbatim; (2) new live evidence sharpens rather than resolves the concern — VP-003 (the one persona whose RAG-mode queries were demonstrably risk-saturated this batch, all 2-3 query slots risk-worded both runs, retrieving explicit suicide/self-harm case content e.g. `qa:1685`/`qa:1730`/`qa:1164`) is also the one persona whose RAG-mode LLM calls failed to produce parseable output in both of its 2 runs (`experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-003/run1/stdout.log`: JSON parse failure; `run2/stdout.log`: schema validation failure, 6 errors). This is a correlation, not a proven causal link — REV-010 (`discussion.md`) concurs with the tracker's own "correlation only, no causal claim" framing — but it is exactly the kind of signal that should keep this precondition from becoming a permanently-dormant, audit-only-if-convenient item.

**Recommendation:** before any further RAG-arm batch, either (a) implement the code-level mitigation (exclude `risk_assessment` from Stage-1 query composition, or summarize/de-risk it before embedding), or (b) if retained as-is, graduate the per-run `retrieval_meta.queries` audit to a mandatory, standing precondition (not optional/post-hoc) for every future RAG batch, and investigate the VP-003 output-reliability failure as a possible downstream consequence of retrieval-driven context complexity (token-budget/prompt-length hypothesis) rather than attributing it purely to unrelated bad luck.

**Linked:** REV-010 (`discussion.md`), EXP-005 (`result.md`)

### Status update | 2026-07-10 | critic (REV-020, EXP-012 evidence review)

**Status:** open — reproduces for the first time on genuinely fresh, non-2026-07-07 F1 text (VP-003), independently confirmed. Stronger evidence for a persona-content-driven mechanism, not a new finding and not a closure.

Every prior reproduction of this finding (EXP-005 through EXP-011) ran against the same fixed ~8 F1 conversation files first generated 2026-07-07. EXP-012 (`result.md`) is the first batch in this project's history to chain a genuinely fresh, live-generated F1 session directly into F2's RAG retrieval — and VP-003's `retrieval_meta.queries` reproduce the exact structural pattern REV-011 §2 predicted, on entirely new specific wording ("살고 싶지 않다, 죽고 싶다는 생각이 매일 계속 든다" / "매일 죽고 싶다는 생각이 지속되며..."), independently re-confirmed directly against the raw artifact `experiments/EXP-012/runs/VP-003/VP-003_20260710_094942_domain_inference.json` (not accepted from `result.md`'s or the report files' prose). The code mitigation (`_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS` excluding `risk_assessment`, REV-011's option (a)) remains unchanged and in effect — the risk-worded content originates in `chief_complaint`/`history_of_present_illness` (the persona's own designed presenting complaint), the two channels REV-011 §2 already identified as structurally unaddressed by that mitigation. This is a legitimate strengthening of the finding (a mechanism confirmed on a second, independently-generated input realization is stronger evidence than any number of re-runs on one fixed transcript), not a new occurrence and not grounds to close this entry. Full reasoning: REV-020 §3 (`discussion.md`).

**Linked:** REV-020 (`discussion.md`), EXP-012 (`result.md`).

### Status update | 2026-07-09 | critic (REV-011, pre-run gate for PLAN-2026-W28-G)

**Status:** open — code mitigation (option a) now shipped in the working tree (`feat/f2-rag-remediation` scope, uncommitted); confirmed correctly implemented but structurally incomplete for VP-003. Not closed by this update — final disposition is deferred to W5 (post-run live evidence review), per this project's own established pattern for this class of finding (VAL-006's REV-007→REV-008→REV-010 arc).

`_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS` (`f2.py:75`) now excludes `risk_assessment` entirely (option (a) from this entry's own Recommendation, not option (b)). Verified by direct code read: `risk_assessment` is composed exclusively from Safety Probe/SI-screen exchange content (`f1.py:360-376`) — 100% risk-topic by construction, so the exclusion loses no legitimate non-risk clinical retrieval signal. However, `chief_complaint`/`history_of_present_illness` — the two slots this mitigation leaves unfiltered — are themselves designed and already observed (EXP-005's own live artifact) to carry equivalent passive-SI content for VP-003 specifically (`docs/ai/personas/VP-003_first_visit_severe.md:129` fixes the persona's own expected `chief_complaint` as `"우울하고... 살고 싶지 않아요"`; EXP-005 run1's live `chief_complaint`/`history_of_present_illness` were both risk-worded). The mitigation therefore closes one of three risk-content channels completely, but does not resolve the underlying retrieval-bias concern for VP-003 — a structural limitation of slot-exclusion for this persona, not an implementation defect. Full reasoning: REV-011 §2 (`discussion.md`).

**Binding condition for the successor RAG re-validation run (REV-011 §3 rule ii):** the mandatory `retrieval_meta.queries` audit must check `chief_complaint`/HPI content against the established broader taxonomy for every RAG-mode VP-003/VP-004 run, not merely confirm `risk_assessment`'s absence from the queries array. This entry remains open until that live audit is performed and its result reported.

**Linked:** REV-011 (`discussion.md`), PLAN-2026-W28-G

### Status update | 2026-07-09 | critic (REV-012, EXP-006 evidence review — W5 gate for PLAN-2026-W28-G)

**Status:** open — stays open, not resolved and not resolved-with-standing-condition. The binding condition REV-011 set (§3 rule ii audit) was executed and discharged for this batch; the underlying concern it was meant to test is not settled.

The mandated `retrieval_meta.queries` audit (REV-011 §3 rule ii) was performed on EXP-006's live RAG-mode runs, independently re-verified by this update: every RAG run's `queries` field was read directly and confirmed to contain no `risk_assessment` text (code mitigation in effect). But VP-003's `chief_complaint`/HPI queries reproduce the exact structural-incompleteness pattern REV-011 §2 predicted, for a **second consecutive batch**: `run1` queries = "살고 싶지 않음, 매일 밤 자살 생각, 수면 장애..." + "...'살아서 뭐하나' 생각 반복" (both risk-worded); `run2` queries = "살고 싶지 않다는 생각이 매일 들어 힘들다고 표현" (identical text, both slots, risk-worded) — both runs again retrieved chunks including explicit suicide/self-harm AI-Hub content (`qa:1685` recurs in both runs' `chunk_ids`, matching EXP-005's own prior retrieval of the same record).

**Why this cannot close, and cannot even graduate to "resolved-with-standing-condition":** the actual empirical question this precondition exists to answer — does the residual retrieval bias contaminate *shipped* RAG evidence for VP-003 — remains genuinely unanswered, not merely unmonitored. Both VP-003 RAG runs this batch produced **zero** domain candidates (LLM-output schema-validation failure, tracked separately as BUG-019) — there is no shipped evidence to check. Two consecutive live batches (EXP-005, EXP-006) have now failed to produce the data point this precondition needs. The standing `retrieval_meta.queries` audit must continue, unconditionally, on every future RAG batch — not because it has caught a violation, but because the one persona it exists to watch has never yet produced a testable result.

Full reasoning, independently re-derived from all 8 RAG artifacts: REV-012 (`discussion.md`) §5.

**Linked:** REV-012 (`discussion.md`), EXP-006 (`result.md`), ADR-015, BUG-019

## [BUG-014] `_RISK_PHRASES` risk-lexicon coverage gap vs. this project's own established passive-SI/burdensomeness taxonomy | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py:76-92` (`_RISK_PHRASES`)
**Linked:** ADR-014, VAL-006, REV-008, BUG-007/ADR-010 (source of the canonical taxonomy), **VAL-009** (critic, same root cause, independently found concurrently in this gate — see cross-check below)
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | `cd apps/ai-server && source .venv/bin/activate` |
| 2 | Read `docs/ai/prompts/safety_classifier/v2.system.md`'s restored 간접 표현 CTRS 하한표 (BUG-007 fix) — canonical anchors: `"살고 싶지 않다", "사라지고 싶다", "없어지고 싶다", "죽고 싶다", "내가 없으면 다 편할 텐데", "사는 게 의미가 없다"` |
| 3 | `python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_014.py -v` |
| 4 | Directly: `python -c "from src.eval.f2_grounding import check_evidence; print(check_evidence('utterance','t','짐이 되기 싫어서 그래요.', chunk_texts={}, utterances={'t':'짐이 되기 싫어서 그래요.'}).verdict)"` |

**Expected:** every phrase in BUG-007/ADR-010's already-established passive-SI/burdensomeness anchor family — the taxonomy this exact project already canonized as one calibration class requiring identical treatment — is rejected by the code-enforced filter ADR-014 introduced specifically to close this rule-2 gap mechanically.

**Actual:** `_RISK_PHRASES` (15 phrases) covers 5 of BUG-007's 6 canonical anchors but has **zero coverage** for `"사는 게 의미가 없다"`. Consequently, REV-008's own live-observed VP-003 run1 evidence quotes from this family — `"짐이 되기 싫어서"` (turn_5/9, burdensomeness) and `"살아서 뭐하나"` (turn_10/12, REV-008's own words: "closely paraphrases the '사는 게 의미가 없다' anchor") — are `verdict=accepted`, not `rejected_risk_lexicon`. A third, standalone phrasing not explicitly enumerated in VAL-009 — `"죽으면 편할 것 같다"` (death-directed ideation; in the actual VP-003 turn_11 artifact this always co-occurs with the already-caught `"사라지고 싶"` phrase in the same quote, so it happens not to leak in that specific run, but a quote containing *only* this phrasing would) — is also `accepted`. All 4 confirmed via `tests/repro/test_bug_014.py` (4/4 pass, demonstrating the pre-fix state per this project's repro-test convention).

**Cross-check against VAL-009 (critic, filed concurrently in this same gate):** VAL-009 independently replayed the actual `VP-003_20260708_120459_domain_inference.json` run1 artifact and found the identical 4 missed quotes (turn_5/9 burdensomeness, turn_10/12 meaninglessness-paraphrase) — same root cause, same phrase families, found by a different method (artifact replay vs. this entry's synthetic-quote code audit + mutation-style lexicon check). This is strong independent corroboration, not a duplicate report: BUG-014 additionally (a) names the missing canonical anchor explicitly (`"사는 게 의미가 없다"`, absent even as a literal string, not just "no paraphrase coverage"), (b) flags the standalone `"죽으면 편할 것 같다"` exposure VAL-009's recommended fix does not explicitly name, and (c) ships an executable pytest regression (`tests/repro/test_bug_014.py`) that a developer fix must flip, giving qa a concrete re-verification path VAL-009 alone does not provide.

**Why this matters for EXP-005 specifically:** PLAN-2026-W28-E step 2 re-runs VP-003 to verify "위반 재발 0 확인" (zero recurrence). VP-003 is the exact persona that produced these exact phrasings live (REV-008). A recurrence of any of the 3 uncaught variants in the live re-run would ship as clean, accepted evidence in `filter_summary`/`whitelist_audit` while reproducing the underlying rule-2 violation this entire v2 remediation mission exists to close — silently, since the artifact would report 0 risk-lexicon rejections for that candidate.

**Minimal repro:** `tests/repro/test_bug_014.py` — `TestBug014RiskLexiconUndercoverage` (4/4 currently PASS, demonstrating the pre-fix/buggy state per this project's established repro-test convention — see BUG-009/BUG-007 precedent for the pass-now/invert-on-fix pattern).

**Proposed fix (not prescribed):** Add stems for the burdensomeness and meaninglessness families to `_RISK_PHRASES` (e.g. `"짐이 되"`/`"부담이 되"`, `"살아서 뭐하"`/`"사는 게 의미"`/`"의미가 없"`-scoped-to-life-context, `"죽으면 편"`), space-insensitive per the existing `_contains_any` convention, matching VAL-009's recommended resolution and additionally covering the standalone death-directed phrasing this entry found. Re-run `tests/repro/test_bug_014.py` after the fix — all 4 assertions should invert to fail (per this project's own convention), at which point qa inverts them per the BUG-007 precedent and marks this resolved.

### QA re-verification | 2026-07-08 | qa (narrow re-gate, `feat/f2-v2-remediation`)

**Status:** open — original 4/4 named quotes independently confirmed fixed; a new, adjacent residual gap found during adjudication. **GATE: FAIL** (see verdict below).
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-08

**1. Suite + lint/type.** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **683 passed, 0 failed** — exact match to developer's claim. `.venv/bin/python -m ruff check` on all 9 touched/new files (`src/eval/f2_grounding.py`, `src/f2.py`, `src/agents/domain_inference.py`, `tests/test_f2_grounding.py`, `tests/test_f2_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_domain_inference.py`, `tests/test_prompt_domain_inference.py`, `tests/repro/test_bug_014.py`, `tests/repro/test_bug_015.py`) → all checks passed. `.venv/bin/python -m mypy src/eval/f2_grounding.py src/f2.py src/agents/domain_inference.py` → no issues in 3 source files.

**2. Repro suites + mutation check.** `tests/repro/test_bug_014.py tests/repro/test_bug_015.py -v` → **11/11 passed** (7 in test_bug_014.py incl. `TestBug014Rev009ArtifactReplay`, 4 in test_bug_015.py). Mutation-checked the load-bearing artifact-replay test (`TestBug014Rev009ArtifactReplay::test_all_broad_taxonomy_quotes_caught_zero_survivors`): monkeypatched `f2_grounding._RISK_PHRASES` in-memory (no file edit) to remove the `"짐이 되"`/`"짐이되"` stems only, re-ran the same replay logic directly — result flipped to **2 survivors** (`VP-003 run1 turn_5`, `turn_9`, both `"...짐이 되기 싫어서."`), confirming the test is genuinely load-bearing on that stem, not vacuously passing. Reverted in-memory (no on-disk change; `git status`/`git diff --stat` on `f2_grounding.py` before/after this check show only developer's own pre-existing uncommitted diff, nothing from this test).

**3. Independent EXP-004 artifact replay (both VP-003 artifacts, re-derived from raw JSON, not by trusting the shipped test's own turn-set).** Loaded `docs/ai/simulation_results/VP-003/VP-003_20260708_120459_domain_inference.json` (run1, 13 evidence items) and `..._120509_domain_inference.json` (run2, 3 evidence items) directly, ran every item through `check_evidence`:
- Run1: turn_0,1,2,3,5,6,9,10,11,12 (10 broad-taxonomy quotes) → all `rejected_risk_lexicon`. turn_4,7,8 (3 non-taxonomy quotes: help-seeking futility / medication-avoidance content) → all `accepted`.
- Run2: turn_0,1,2 (3 broad-taxonomy quotes) → all `rejected_risk_lexicon`.
- **Total: 13/13 broad-taxonomy quotes caught, 0 survivors. 3/3 non-taxonomy quotes still accepted (no over-blocking regression).** Matches developer's claim exactly, independently re-derived.

**4. Disjointness / panic-idiom / VP-003 fixtures / cascade-pipeline.** `tests/test_f2_grounding.py tests/test_f2_pipeline.py tests/test_domain_inference.py tests/test_prompt_domain_inference.py` → **108/108 passed**, including `test_risk_and_panic_idiom_lexicons_are_disjoint`, `test_panic_idiom_quote_accepted`/`test_panic_idiom_vp004_style_quote_accepted`/`test_panic_idiom_without_symptom_context_not_risk_rejected`/`test_panic_idiom_co_occurring_with_genuine_si_still_rejected` (both-ways idiom coverage), `test_vp003_run1_passive_si_quote_rejected`/`test_vp003_run2_passive_si_quote_rejected` (exact-quote fixtures), and the full `TestRejectionCascade` (5 tests) + `TestCascadeWiredIntoArtifact` (3 tests) — untouched by this fix, still green.

**5. Residual adjudication — "차라리 죽는 게 낫다"/"죽는 게 낫다" (REV-009 Attack 4, developer's flagged out-of-scope item).** Tested directly against the current (post-fix) lexicon via `check_evidence`, three independent synthetic phrasings:
```
'차라리 죽는 게 나을 것 같아요.'              → verdict=accepted
'이렇게 힘들게 사느니 죽는 게 낫다는 생각이 들어요.' → verdict=accepted
'죽는 게 낫겠다 싶어요.'                      → verdict=accepted
```
Confirmed: this construct survives as accepted evidence today, exactly as developer flagged and exactly as REV-009 Attack 4 found ("contains neither a `_RISK_PHRASES` nor a `_PANIC_IDIOM_PHRASES` match... would simply ship as unflagged accepted evidence"). Cross-checked `src/f2.py` for any reference to `safety_classifier`/`crisis_protocol`/`CRISIS_RESPONSE` — none found; F2/domain-inference evidence-citation is architecturally decoupled from the crisis-routing pipeline, so this specific gap's downstream consequence is a domain-inference evidence-citation validity issue (rule-2/ADR-014 enforcement), not a missed crisis-intervention routing failure.

**Judgment: add the stem now — GATE: FAIL, blocking on this one item.** Reasoning:
- **Cost is trivial.** One more stem (e.g. `"죽는 게 낫"`/`"차라리 죽는"`, space-insensitive, same `_contains_any` convention already used for all 33 existing entries) — the same class of fix developer just made four of, in the same file, in the same sitting.
- **Same review, same disposition class, inconsistent treatment.** REV-009 Attack 4 explicitly named this exact phrasing and explicitly folded it into the same "general lexicon-coverage gap" disposition as Attack 1's four items ("Folded into Attack 1/3's broader-taxonomy list above rather than filed as a distinct ISS-046 defect"). Developer fixed all four Attack-1-sourced items under that disposition but left this fifth, same-disposition item out as "explicitly out of scope" — there is no stated reason the cost/benefit differs for this one item.
- **The compensating control has a demonstrated failure mode in this exact project.** REV-009's proposed backstop (Attack 3's secondary manual audit, "mandatory," binding for EXP-005) is real and specifically itemizes this phrase family by name — not a vague gesture. But this project's own error.md shows manual-audit arithmetic/counting slips are a recurring pattern (VAL-002: 4/4 cited per-turn CTRS figures wrong; VAL-008: evidence-quote sums wrong twice in the same entry) — relying solely on a human catching one more named phrase among many accepted quotes across all VPs/both arms, when a one-line deterministic fix is available and cheap, is a weaker safety margin than closing the hole mechanically.
- **The persona under direct test is the one already shown to generate this exact paraphrase family unprompted.** VP-003 (DATASET-004-flagged for extra scrutiny) already produced two prior unprompted paraphrases of the same "better off dead"/burdensomeness construct family (turn_5/9, turn_10/12) that required this very BUG-014 fix — a third natural paraphrase in the same family, in the same persona's next live run, is a plausible outcome, not a hypothetical edge case.
- This is a QA gate judgment on closing a known, cheap, already-adjudicated-adjacent hole before live spend — it does not reopen or override REV-009's own separate research-validity verdict (REV-009 remains NON-BLOCKING as written, for the state it reviewed); it is an independent bar this gate sets before EXP-005 runs.

**Verdict:** BUG-014 stays **open**. Developer must add one stem for the "죽는 게 낫"/"차라리 죽는" construct to `_RISK_PHRASES`, then qa re-verifies (invert the corresponding assertion in a `test_bug_014.py` addition, per the established convention) before this gate can pass.

### QA final re-verification (residual closed) | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Status:** resolved
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-08 (final sign-off on `feat/f2-v2-remediation`, in response to the "GATE: FAIL pending one more stem" verdict above)

Developer added the demanded stem. Independently re-verified, item by item:

1. **Suite.** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **687 passed, 0 failed** — exact match to developer's claim (683 + 4 new, all in the new `TestBug014QaReverificationResidual` class).

Its 3 parametrized `_QA_BETTER_OFF_DEAD_VARIANTS` quotes are byte-for-byte identical to the 3 synthetic phrasings this gate's own prior "Residual adjudication" section tested and reported `accepted` pre-fix:
   - `"차라리 죽는 게 나을 것 같아요."`
   - `"이렇게 힘들게 사느니 죽는 게 낫다는 생각이 들어요."`
   - `"죽는 게 낫겠다 싶어요."`
   All 3 now assert `verdict == VERDICT_REJECTED_RISK_LEXICON` and pass. A 4th test in the same class (`test_panic_idioms_still_accepted_both_ways`) re-checks the ISS-046 panic fear-of-dying idiom quote stays `VERDICT_ACCEPTED` — confirming the new stems don't collide with `죽는 줄 알았`.

## [BUG-015] `_RISK_PHRASES` over-blocks on bare, context-free `"손목"`/`"목숨"` entries — benign clinical quotes silently rejected | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py:76-92` (`_RISK_PHRASES`)
**Linked:** ADR-014, PLAN-2026-W28-E step 1g gate requirement ("사전리뷰: ... 관용구 오차단 여부")
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | `cd apps/ai-server && source .venv/bin/activate` |
| 2 | `python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_015.py -v` |
| 3 | Directly: `python -c "from src.eval.f2_grounding import check_evidence; print(check_evidence('utterance','t','요즘 손목이 계속 아파서 타이핑도 힘들어요.', chunk_texts={}, utterances={'t':'요즘 손목이 계속 아파서 타이핑도 힘들어요.'}, domain='trauma').verdict)"` |

**Expected:** the risk-lexicon filter rejects only quotes whose meaning is genuinely SI/self-harm/death-directed. A wrist-pain somatic complaint or a car-accident-survivor narrative ("목숨을 건졌다" = "my life was saved" — semantically the *opposite* of suicidal ideation) must not be treated as risk content.

**Actual:** `_RISK_PHRASES` includes two bare, short, polysemous entries — `"손목"` ("wrist") and `"목숨"` ("life"/"one's life") — with no co-occurring-context requirement, unlike every other entry (multi-word, SI-directed phrases: `"살고 싶지 않"`, `"죽고 싶"`, `"자해"`, etc.) and unlike the ISS-046 panic-idiom carve-out (`is_panic_idiom_evidence`), which structurally requires an idiom phrase AND physical-symptom context before excusing fear-of-dying language. As a result:
- `"요즘 손목이 계속 아파서 타이핑도 힘들어요."` (wrist-pain somatic complaint, any domain) → `rejected_risk_lexicon`.
- `"교통사고 났을 때 정말 목숨을 건졌다고 생각해요."` (car-accident survivor narrative, `trauma` domain) → `rejected_risk_lexicon`.
- `"이번 발표를 목숨 걸고 준비했어요."` (idiomatic-effort expression, no clinical risk content at all) → `rejected_risk_lexicon`.

Consequence at the cascade level (`filter_domain_candidates`): a `trauma` domain candidate whose SOLE evidence is the survivor-narrative quote above is eliminated from the artifact entirely (`DomainCandidate.evidence` `min_length=1` + "no evidence, no candidate") — a silent false-negative candidate loss for a patient accurately describing surviving a traumatic event, the opposite failure direction from BUG-014/VAL-009 (under-blocking) but from the same root design gap: single-word lexicon entries with no context guard.

**Minimal repro:** `tests/repro/test_bug_015.py` — `TestBug015RiskLexiconOverBlocking` (4/4 currently PASS, demonstrating the pre-fix/buggy over-blocking state per this project's established repro-test convention — see BUG-009/BUG-007 precedent).

**Proposed fix (not prescribed):** Either (a) replace the bare `"손목"`/`"목숨"` entries with multi-word, SI-specific phrasings (e.g. `"손목을 그"`/`"손목 그"` for self-harm-by-cutting mentions, `"목숨을 끊"`/`"목숨을 버리"` for suicide-directed usage), analogous to how every other entry in the list is already multi-word and directional; or (b) give them the same structural context-requirement pattern the ISS-046 panic-idiom carve-out already uses (require a co-occurring self-harm-directed verb/context word, not just the bare noun). Re-run `tests/repro/test_bug_015.py` after the fix — all 4 assertions should invert to fail, at which point qa inverts them per the BUG-007 precedent and marks this resolved.

### Resolution

| Field | Value |
|:--|:--|
| Fixed by | uncommitted working tree, `feat/f2-v2-remediation` (`apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py`) |
| Fix approach | option (a) — bare `"손목"`/`"목숨"` removed; replaced with multi-word, SI-directed stems `"손목을 긋"`/`"손목을긋"`/`"손목을 그"`/`"손목을그"` (self-harm-by-cutting) and `"목숨을 끊"`/`"목숨을끊"`/`"목숨을 버리"`/`"목숨을버리"` (suicide-directed), following the ISS-038 multi-word-stem convention already used elsewhere in this codebase |
| Regression test | `apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_015.py::TestBug015RiskLexiconOverBlocking` (4/4 pass, assertions now assert the fixed/accepted behavior) |
| Verified by | qa, 2026-07-08 |

**Independent re-verification (this gate, not just re-running the shipped test):** ran the exact 3 quotes from this entry's own Reproduction section directly through `check_evidence`:
```
'요즘 손목이 계속 아파서 타이핑도 힘들어요.'          (wrist pain, any domain)     → accepted
'교통사고 났을 때 정말 목숨을 건졌다고 생각해요.'      (accident survivor, trauma)  → accepted
'이번 발표를 목숨 걸고 준비했어요.'                   (idiomatic effort)           → accepted
```
All three benign quotes now `accepted`, matching the fix's intent exactly. Also checked the fix does not swing to under-blocking (the opposite failure BUG-014 addresses): genuine self-harm-by-cutting (`"손목을 긋고 싶다는 충동이 들어요."`) and suicide-directed (`"목숨을 끊고 싶다는 생각을 해요."`) phrasings both correctly still `rejected_risk_lexicon`. Full suite (683 passed), ruff/mypy clean on `f2_grounding.py` (see BUG-014's QA re-verification note for the shared gate run). No regression in `TestRiskLexiconFilter`/`TestRejectionCascade`/cascade-pipeline tests (108/108 passed, see BUG-014 note).

**Status:** resolved — fully verified, no residual concerns.

## [BUG-016] `chunk_id=` source_id prefix echo — RAG-arm evidence over-rejected as `rejected_unknown_source` | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py:75,77` (root cause — prompt wording); `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py:247-254` (`check_evidence`'s exact-match source_id lookup)
**Affected EXP:** EXP-005 RAG arm (`result.md`) — VP-003 rows already disclosed as uninterpretable independent of this bug; this bug affects VP-001/run1, VP-002/run1, VP-002/run2 (10/35 would-be-accepted `rag_chunk` evidence items, ~29%)
**Linked:** REV-010 (finding 1, `discussion.md`), ADR-015 (RAG arm remains EXPERIMENTAL, condition 1), VAL-010 (adjacent, not caused by this bug)
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | `cd apps/ai-server && source .venv/bin/activate` |
| 2 | `python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_016.py -v` |
| 3 | Directly: `python -c "from src.eval.f2_grounding import check_evidence; print(check_evidence('rag_chunk', 'chunk_id=case_card:687', '<real quote>', chunk_texts={'case_card:687': '<real quote>'}, utterances={}, domain='depression').verdict)"` → `rejected_unknown_source` |

**Root cause.** `DomainInferenceAgent._build_user_content` (`src/agents/domain_inference.py:75`) headers the retrieved-chunk block with the literal text `"## 검색된 근거 청크 (source_type=rag_chunk, source_id=chunk_id)"`, and `:77` lists each chunk as `f"- chunk_id={c.chunk_id} [{c.source_type}]: {c.text}"` — the literal string `chunk_id=` sits directly adjacent to the ID in the text the model reads. Solar Pro3 sometimes echoes that literal label into the `source_id` field it emits in its JSON response (e.g. `"chunk_id=case_card:687"` instead of `"case_card:687"`). `check_evidence` (`f2_grounding.py:247-254`) does an exact-match lookup, `chunk_texts.get(source_id)`, with no normalization — the prefixed ID is not a key in `chunk_texts`, so the item is rejected `rejected_unknown_source`, even though the underlying chunk and quote are both genuinely real and retrieved that run. Both root-cause line numbers independently re-verified by qa (confirmed at `domain_inference.py:75,77` exactly as REV-010 cited).

**Expected:** a `rag_chunk` evidence item whose cited chunk was actually retrieved this run, and whose quote is lexically grounded in that chunk's text, is accepted regardless of a benign source_id formatting variation the model introduces.

**Actual:** confirmed live in EXP-005 (RAG arm, first activation, 2026-07-08) — all 10 of that batch's `rejected_unknown_source` verdicts (`rag/VP-001/run1` ×6, `rag/VP-002/run1` ×2, `rag/VP-002/run2` ×2) carry the exact `chunk_id=` prefix. Independently re-traced by qa against the real artifact `experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-001/run1/VP-001_20260708_200259_domain_inference.json` (gitignored, not depended on by the repro test): `whitelist_audit.verdicts[0]` has `source_id="chunk_id=case_card:687"`, `verdict="rejected_unknown_source"`; the stripped ID `"case_card:687"` is present in that run's own `retrieval_meta.chunk_ids`, and the quote text matches `chunk_texts["case_card:687"]` verbatim — genuine, well-grounded evidence dropped for a formatting reason only. This is a systematic over-rejection: **~29% of the RAG arm's `rag_chunk` evidence this batch (10/35 would-be-accepted items)**. Direction is conservative (false negative, not fabrication) and did not eliminate any candidate this batch — every affected domain retained ≥1 other accepted item — but `tests/repro/test_bug_016.py::TestBug016ChunkIdPrefixEcho::test_cascade_eliminates_candidate_when_sole_evidence_is_prefixed` demonstrates the downstream risk REV-010 flagged: a domain candidate whose SOLE evidence item carries this prefix is fully eliminated by `filter_domain_candidates`'s "no evidence, no candidate" cascade (`DomainCandidate.evidence` `min_length=1`), which could happen in a sparser-evidence run.

**Minimal repro:** `tests/repro/test_bug_016.py` — `TestBug016ChunkIdPrefixEcho` (4/4 currently PASS, demonstrating the pre-fix/buggy over-rejection state, per this project's established repro-test convention — BUG-007/BUG-009/BUG-014/BUG-015 precedent). Test 1 reproduces the exact rejection on real (verbatim-copied) artifact data; test 2 is a control confirming the same chunk/quote is accepted without the prefix (isolating the defect to format, not content); test 3 demonstrates the normalization-strip fix option as a self-contained oracle (no src/ change); test 4 demonstrates the cascade-elimination risk at the candidate level.

**Proposed fix (not prescribed — two options, per REV-010's own resolution table):**
(a) **Prompt wording** — reword `domain_inference.py:75,77`'s per-chunk listing so the literal string `chunk_id=` does not appear directly adjacent to the ID (e.g. `f"- [{c.chunk_id}] ({c.source_type}): {c.text}"` with a header that doesn't say `source_id=chunk_id`). Cheap, but not deterministic — depends on the model actually following the reworded format, and prompt-only fixes are exactly the enforcement class this project's own history (VAL-006/REV-008, ADR-014) found unreliable for a different rule.
(b) **Code-level normalization (deterministic — recommended)** — in `check_evidence` (`f2_grounding.py`, before the `chunk_texts.get(source_id)` lookup at line 248), defensively strip a leading `chunk_id=` (and plausible variants, e.g. `chunk_id = `) from `source_id` when `source_type == "rag_chunk"` before the lookup. This mirrors the project's existing "code-enforce, don't trust the prompt alone" pattern (ADR-014's own rule-2 code enforcement). `tests/repro/test_bug_016.py::test_stripping_the_prefix_recovers_acceptance` demonstrates the exact transform (`source_id.removeprefix("chunk_id=")`) as a ready-to-port oracle.

Re-run `tests/repro/test_bug_016.py` after the fix — the 4 assertions above should invert (tests 1 and 4 should flip from rejected/eliminated to accepted/survives), at which point qa inverts them per the BUG-007 precedent and marks this resolved.

### Resolution

| Field | Value |
|:--|:--|
| Fixed by | uncommitted working tree, `feat/f2-rag-remediation` (PLAN-2026-W28-G T1-dev, base `9d857fe`) |
| Fix approach | option (b), code-level (deterministic) + option (a) as defense-in-depth. `_normalize_rag_chunk_source_id()` (new, `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py:186-215`, regex `^\s*chunk_id\s*=\s*`) strips a leading `chunk_id=` prefix (whitespace-tolerant around `=`) from a `rag_chunk` source_id once, before the `chunk_texts` lookup in `check_evidence` (`f2_grounding.py:280`); the normalized id is used for both the lookup and the recorded `EvidenceVerdict.source_id`. `_build_user_content` (`apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py:75,77`) reworded so the literal string `chunk_id=` no longer sits adjacent to the id (`f"- [{c.chunk_id}] ({c.source_type}): {c.text}"`), plus a new prompt rule-4 sentence in `docs/ai/prompts/domain_inference/v2.system.md` ("source_id는 대괄호 안 값을 접두사 없이 그대로 복사한다"). |
| Regression tests | `apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_016.py::TestBug016ChunkIdPrefixEcho` (4/4, inverted from pre-fix rejected/eliminated assertions to post-fix accepted/survives, per BUG-007 convention) + `apps/ai-server/tests/test_f2_grounding.py::TestBug016ChunkIdPrefixNormalization` (new, 6 tests: exact-prefix strip, whitespace variants, unprefixed-unchanged, only-leading-prefix-stripped, `check_evidence` acceptance, `utterance`-scope exclusion) |
| Verified by | qa, 2026-07-09 |

**Independent re-verification (this gate — not a re-run of the developer's own claimed numbers):**

1. **Suite.** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **707 passed, 0 failed** — exact match to developer's claim.
2. **Repro + regression tests, read in full (not just executed).** `tests/repro/test_bug_016.py -v` → 4/4 PASS. `tests/test_f2_grounding.py -k Bug016` → 6/6 PASS. Confirmed non-vacuous by reading assertions: test 1 asserts `v.verdict == VERDICT_ACCEPTED` AND `v.source_id == _REAL_CHUNK_ID` (the normalized id, not the raw prefixed one) on the exact production-artifact-derived prefixed input; test 4 asserts the candidate SURVIVES the cascade (`len(filtered) == 1`) while its raw `evidence[0].source_id` stays the model's un-normalized string (`"chunk_id=case_card:687"`) — confirming design note (1) directly (see item 4 below).
3. **Mutation check (load-bearing, in-memory only, no on-disk change).** Patched `src.eval.f2_grounding._normalize_rag_chunk_source_id` to an identity no-op via `unittest.mock.patch.object` and re-ran `test_prefixed_source_id_accepted_after_normalization_fix()` directly: verdict flipped to `rejected_unknown_source` and the assertion raised `AssertionError` as expected. Reverted (context manager exit, no disk write); re-ran the same test — passes again. Confirms the test is genuinely load-bearing on the normalization function, not vacuously true. Also confirmed via direct interpreter call (no patch): monkeypatching the module attribute to identity and calling `check_evidence` directly reproduces `rejected_unknown_source`; restoring reproduces `accepted`.
4. **Deterministic + rag_chunk-scoped, utterance untouched.** Read `check_evidence` (`f2_grounding.py:255-300`): the normalization call (`source_id = _normalize_rag_chunk_source_id(source_id)`) sits strictly inside the `if source_type == "rag_chunk":` branch (line 275-280); the `elif source_type == "utterance":` branch (line 288-295) never calls it. `test_check_evidence_normalization_is_rag_chunk_only` (in the new `TestBug016ChunkIdPrefixNormalization` class) independently confirms this: `check_evidence("utterance", "chunk_id=turn_0", ...)` still returns `rejected_unknown_source` (a `turn_id` is never legitimately prefixed, so no normalization is applied there — correct scope). The regex itself (`^\s*chunk_id\s*=\s*`, anchored at start, `count=1` in `.sub()`) is deterministic and idempotent-safe (an already-normalized id with no prefix passes through unchanged, verified by `test_normalize_leaves_unprefixed_id_unchanged`).
5. **Design-note (1) confirmed accurate, and flagged (not blocking) for EXP-audit clarity.** Developer's claim — artifact `DomainCandidate.evidence[].source_id` keeps the model's raw (possibly prefixed) string, while the verdict/lookup use the normalized id — is confirmed exactly by `test_cascade_no_longer_eliminates_candidate_when_sole_evidence_is_prefixed`'s own explicit assertion (`filtered[0].evidence[0].source_id == f"chunk_id={_REAL_CHUNK_ID}"`) and by direct code read: `filter_domain_candidates` passes through the original, un-mutated `DomainCandidate`/`DomainEvidence` objects the model produced — `check_evidence` only reads `evidence.source_id`, it never writes the normalized value back onto the `DomainEvidence` object itself. **Net effect flagged for the record:** a future artifact reader cross-referencing `domain_candidates[].evidence[].source_id` against `whitelist_audit.verdicts[].source_id` for the "same" evidence item may see two different strings for a prefixed item (raw vs. normalized) — intentional (preserves audit fidelity of what the model actually said) and acceptable, but worth a one-line note in any future DR/EXP write-up that cites both fields together, so a reader does not mistake it for an inconsistency.
6. **ruff/mypy.** `ruff check` on all 4 touched src files + 5 touched test files → all checks passed. `mypy src/eval/f2_grounding.py src/agents/domain_inference.py src/f2.py src/schemas/domain_inference.py` → no issues (23 pre-existing repo-wide mypy errors confirmed unrelated — independently re-run, none in the 4 touched files).
7. **Diff-scope.** `git status` (repo root) shows exactly the 10 files developer claimed (4 src + 5 test + 1 prompt doc), plus one unrelated pre-existing untracked dir (`analysis/`, data agent's DATASET-005 leakage-audit script, mtime ~10 min before this diff's earliest file — not part of this PR). `stat` mtimes on the 10 changed files cluster within a 400-second window (1783561029–1783561429), confirming a single coherent edit session with no scope creep.
8. **Leakage-audit sanity (data-leakage-audit skill).** `grep -n "gold\|ground_truth\|\blabel\b"` and `grep -n "accuracy\|f1\|auc\|\bscore\b|evaluate"` across all 4 touched src files → no hits indicating ground-truth/metric leakage into model code; the one `\blabel\b`-pattern hit and the one `score=` hit are both pre-existing, unrelated code (a log-message word and a RAG-chunk relevance-score field, not part of this diff). No VP persona name/golden-quote hardcoded into src/ as a literal driving test-passing logic (VP-003/VP-004 references found are all explanatory code comments citing bug history, consistent with this project's established convention).

**GATE: PASS.** BUG-016 closes as resolved — code-level normalization verified deterministic, rag_chunk-scoped, mutation-checked non-vacuous, and the design note re: artifact-vs-verdict source_id divergence confirmed accurate (flagged above for future EXP-audit awareness, not blocking).

### Closure confirmation (RAG-arm certification lift) | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Status:** resolved — reconfirmed, no change to disposition.
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (per critic REV-015 Part A §A.5/§A.7, "BUG-016 may all close" authorization)

Independently re-checked against the EXP-008 artifacts (not accepted from REV-015's or `result.md`'s prose): `whitelist_audit.counts.rejected_unknown_source == 0` on all 4 `experiments/EXP-008/runs/{VP-003,VP-001}/{run1,run2}/**/*_domain_inference.json` files (direct `json.load` read, this gate). No new BUG/VAL required. ## [BUG-017] VP-003 RAG-mode LLM-output reliability — 2/2 EXP-005 runs failed to produce parseable output | 2026-07-08 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py:128-143` (`_call`, hardcoded `max_tokens=1536`); `apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py:117-123` (`_parse`)
**Affected EXP:** EXP-005 RAG arm (`result.md`) — VP-003/run1 and VP-003/run2 (both RAG-mode) — result.md's EXP-005 entry and REV-010 already disclose these two rows as uninterpretable (0-candidate, LLM-output failure, not a scoring/filter finding); this BUG formalizes and further investigates that disclosed finding
**Linked:** REV-010 (finding 2, `discussion.md`), ADR-015 (RAG arm remains EXPERIMENTAL, condition 2), VAL-010 (correlation noted, not causation — VP-003's Stage-1 queries are the most risk-topic-saturated in the batch)
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | Artifact evidence (no live LLM re-run performed by qa — none available/in charter): `experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-003/run1/stdout.log`, `experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-003/run2/stdout.log` (gitignored; verbatim lines quoted below) |
| 2 | Code-path repro (no live LLM): `cd apps/ai-server && source .venv/bin/activate && python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_017.py -v` |

**Actual (artifact evidence, verbatim):**
- run1: `src.agents.domain_inference WARNING DomainInference parse failure: LLM response JSON parse failure: Unterminated string starting at: line 157 column 20 (char 5053)`
- run2: `src.agents.domain_inference WARNING DomainInference parse failure: LLM response schema validation failure: 6 error(s)`

Both preceded by a successful Stage 1 (`f2.stage1.ok — mode=rag, chunks=11` / `chunks=6`, same stdout.log files) — the failure is Stage 2 (LLM output) only. `filter_summary.candidates_before=0` for both (confirmed directly in `experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-003/run{1,2}/*_domain_inference.json`) — the risk-lexicon cascade never ran because there was never a parseable candidate to cascade.

**Expected:** VP-003 — this project's own governing chain's (VAL-006/REV-008/DATASET-004) highest-scrutiny persona — produces at least one clean, parseable RAG-mode run so its risk≠domain behavior can actually be evaluated under RAG mode (it has never been, at any point in this project, per REV-010).

**Fail-safe verified correct (not itself a defect):** `DomainInferenceAgent.run`'s degrade-on-parse-failure path (`_parse` → `None, reason` → `DomainInferenceOutput` with empty candidates, no crash, `mode` still honestly `rag`) is confirmed correct by direct code read (`domain_inference.py:226-237`) and by both the pre-existing generic test (`tests/test_domain_inference.py::TestAgentRuntime::test_run_degrades_gracefully_on_json_parse_failure` / `..._on_schema_validation_failure`) and this BUG's own repro reproducing the real failure *shapes* (`tests/repro/test_bug_017.py::TestBug017TruncatedJsonReproShape`, unterminated-string-mid-JSON and valid-JSON/invalid-schema respectively, not just the generic `"not json at all"` fixture).

**Leading hypothesis (REV-010, qa-verified config value, not confirmed as cause):** `max_tokens=1536` (`domain_inference.py:140`, confirmed by qa via direct code read AND via `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py::TestBug017MaxTokensConfig::test_call_passes_max_tokens_1536`, which asserts the live value through the actual `_call` code path, not just a source-line grep) — the same fixed ceiling applies to VP-003's heavier RAG-mode prompt (11 retrieved chunks this run) as to a bare llm_only call with zero chunks; no per-mode budget increase exists in code. Order-of-magnitude plausibility check (not proof, no solar-pro3 tokenizer available in this environment): 1536 completion tokens at a typical ~2-4 chars/token ratio for mixed Korean+JSON content spans roughly 3000-6100 characters, bracketing char 5053 — consistent with, but not proof of, truncation. This matches REV-010's own "correlation, not established causation" framing (VP-003's Stage-1 queries this batch were also the most risk-topic-saturated in the dataset, per VAL-010, retrieving explicit suicide/self-harm case content — a plausible driver of a longer/harder-to-format response, but not established).

**New finding (qa, beyond REV-010's original scope) — the truncation hypothesis is currently unfalsifiable from this project's own artifacts.** `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py::TestBug017FinishReasonDiscarded` demonstrates: `ChatResponse` (`src/adapters/base.py:20-27`) carries `finish_reason` and `usage` fields, and `SolarPro3Adapter.chat` populates both (`src/adapters/solar_pro3.py:100-113`, `finish_reason=choice.finish_reason` + a `completion_tokens` usage dict) — `finish_reason == "length"` is the OpenAI-compatible API's own direct signal that a completion was cut off by the token budget. But `DomainInferenceAgent._call` (`domain_inference.py:128-143`) returns only `(resp.content, resp.model, resp.latency_ms)`, discarding both fields, and `DomainInferenceOutput` (`src/schemas/domain_inference.py:125-132`) has no field for either. **Neither the two failed VP-003 runs, nor any future re-run, can have this hypothesis confirmed or ruled out from this pipeline's own logs/artifacts as currently instrumented** — the one signal that would settle it is dropped before it ever reaches a log line or the output schema.

**Minimal repro:** `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py` (4/4 tests currently PASS — config-value confirmation, both real failure-shape reproductions via the actual `_parse`/`run` code path, and the finish_reason/usage-discarded diagnostic-gap demonstration). No live LLM call is made or available to qa; the actual VP-003 RAG n=2 clean re-verification REV-010's resolution #1 calls for remains a developer/experiment-tracker follow-up with live LLM access, outside qa's charter (no src/ changes, no live LLM here) — this entry is evidence + code-path repro + config verification, not a live re-run.

**Proposed fix / next steps (not prescribed):**
(a) Investigate raising `max_tokens` for the RAG-mode call path specifically (VP-003's RAG prompt is materially longer than llm_only's), or truncating/summarizing the risk-heavy retrieved-chunk content before it reaches the prompt (this would also partially address VAL-010's standing concern about risk-topic-biased retrieval).
(b) **Fast-follow, low-cost, orthogonal to (a):** capture and log `finish_reason`/`usage` on every LLM call (success or parse-failure) so a future truncation hypothesis is falsifiable from this project's own logs, not just inferred from a character offset. `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py::TestBug017FinishReasonDiscarded::test_finish_reason_and_usage_not_captured_anywhere` is written to invert (fail, then be updated to assert presence) once this is implemented.
(c) Re-run VP-003 RAG n=2 cleanly (REV-010 resolution #1) once (a) is attempted, and pass the secondary risk-lexicon audit, before any RAG-arm claim about VP-003 is made (ADR-015 condition).

### Phase A verification (instrumentation only) — qa | 2026-07-09

**Status:** open — phase A (fast-follow (b) above) verified complete and correct; phase A does NOT close this BUG. Root cause (max_tokens truncation vs. an unrelated cause) is still undiagnosed, phase B (a) is not attempted, and a clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-verification (c) has not been run. Stays **open** per this gate's brief (deliberately, not an oversight).
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09

Developer implemented fast-follow (b) only, as scoped by HANDOFF/PLAN-2026-W28-G T1-dev ("BUG-017 phase A"). Independently re-verified:

1. **`max_tokens` still 1536 — phase B not done prematurely.** `grep -n max_tokens apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py` → single hit, `max_tokens=1536,` (line 159), unchanged from pre-fix. Confirmed by direct read of `_call` (lines 139-162) — the only change to `_call`'s LLM-invocation call is its return signature/tuple, not any argument passed to `adapter.chat_timed`.
2. **`finish_reason`/`usage` genuinely captured, not just declared.** Read `_call` (returns `(resp.content, resp.model, resp.latency_ms, resp.finish_reason, resp.usage)`, was a 3-tuple pre-fix) and `run()` (both the primary call site, line 204, and the fallback call site, line 232, unpack all 5 elements and thread `finish_reason`/`usage` through). This is a genuine plumbing change, not a cosmetic field addition — verified the values flow from the real adapter response object, not a hardcoded default, via `tests/test_domain_inference.py::TestAgentRuntime::test_run_captures_and_logs_finish_reason_and_usage_on_success` (constructs a mock `ChatResponse` with `finish_reason="stop"`/real `usage` dict, asserts `out.finish_reason`/`out.usage` match) and `..._on_parse_failure` (same, with `finish_reason="length"`/`usage={"completion_tokens": 1536, ...}` on an unparseable response — i.e. the exact shape of VP-003's real failures). Both PASS: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_domain_inference.py -k finish_reason -v` → **2/2 passed**.
3. **Logged on BOTH success AND parse/schema-failure paths.** Read `run()` lines 250-273: the `if parsed is None:` branch (parse/schema failure) logs `"DomainInference parse failure: %s (model=%s, finish_reason=%s, usage=%s)"` at WARNING before returning; the success branch below it logs `"DomainInference call ok — model=%s, finish_reason=%s, usage=%s"` at INFO. Both confirmed to actually fire (not just present in source) by the two tests in item 2 above, which use `caplog` to assert on the real log records emitted during a real `agent.run()` call, not by string-matching the source file. **Transport-failure path (exception before any `ChatResponse` exists) correctly does NOT log finish_reason/usage** (there is none to log — `finish_reason`/`usage` stay `None`, consistent with the schema docstring's own "None whenever no ChatResponse was ever obtained" contract) — this is correct behavior, not a gap, and is distinct from the parse/schema-failure path (which DOES have a real `ChatResponse`, and IS logged).
4. **Persisted into the output schema.** `DomainInferenceOutput` (`src/schemas/domain_inference.py`) gains `finish_reason: str | None` / `usage: dict[str, int] | None`, both `default=None` (non-breaking for any existing caller). Confirmed both fields are actually set on every `DomainInferenceOutput` construction in `run()` that has a real LLM response (both the parse-failure return, line 260-268, and the success return, line 274-286) — read directly, not assumed from the field declaration alone.
5. **Persisted into the artifact (`f2.py`).** `_repro_metadata()` gains `finish_reason`/`usage` kwargs, written into the `repro` dict; `_build_artifact()` mirrors both at the artifact's top level alongside `model_used`/`prompt_version`/`latency_ms`; `_build_report()`'s markdown table adds `finish_reason`/`usage` rows using `repro.get(...)` (not `repro[...]`, so a pre-fix repro dict without these keys would not `KeyError` — a defensive, backward-compatible choice, confirmed by reading the code). `_run()`'s call site passes `finish_reason=output.finish_reason, usage=output.usage` into `_repro_metadata()` — traced end-to-end from `DomainInferenceOutput` to the artifact JSON's `repro`/top-level `finish_reason`/`usage` keys, and to the markdown report table, with no dropped hop.
6. **Mutation check (load-bearing, in-memory only, no on-disk change).** Patched `DomainInferenceAgent._call` (via `unittest.mock.patch.object`) to call the real `_call` internally but then discard `finish_reason`/`usage` before returning (simulating the exact pre-fix discard behavior at the source), and re-ran `test_run_captures_and_logs_finish_reason_and_usage_on_success` directly: the test failed with `AssertionError: BUG-017 phase A regression: DomainInferenceOutput.finish_reason no longer carries the adapter's finish_reason`, confirming the test is genuinely load-bearing on the real plumbing, not vacuously true. Reverted (context manager exit, no disk write); re-ran — passes again.
7. **`max_tokens=1536` truncation hypothesis is now genuinely falsifiable, as claimed.** The specific gap qa's original BUG-017 filing identified ("neither the two failed VP-003 runs, nor any future re-run, can have this hypothesis confirmed or ruled out from this pipeline's own logs/artifacts as currently instrumented") is closed for FUTURE runs — a future clean VP-003 RAG re-run (item (c) above) will now emit `finish_reason` in its logs and artifact, directly answering whether truncation occurred (`finish_reason == "length"`) without inference from a character offset. This does **not** retroactively diagnose the two already-failed EXP-005 runs (`experiments/EXP-005/runs/rag/VP-003/run{1,2}`, gitignored) — their `stdout.log`s predate this instrumentation and were not re-run (no live LLM access in this gate, per charter).
8. **Suite, ruff/mypy, diff-scope.** Covered jointly with BUG-016 above (same PR): 707 passed / 0 failed; ruff clean; mypy clean on the 4 touched src files; 10-file diff-scope confirmed via `git status` + mtime clustering, no scope creep.

**Why this stays open (per the brief's explicit instruction, not a gate failure):** phase A is diagnostic instrumentation only — it makes the truncation hypothesis testable, it does not test it. VP-003 RAG mode has still never produced a single clean, parseable result at any point in this project. Closing this BUG requires phase B (a root-cause fix, whichever the diagnostic run points to — raised `max_tokens`, prompt trimming, or something else) AND a clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-run passing the secondary risk-lexicon audit (ADR-015 condition 2/3). Recommend the next mission's experiment-tracker run the BUG-017 diagnostic dispatch (PLAN-2026-W28-G T2-exp, "BUG-017 진단런") next, now that phase A is verified ready to receive it.

**Status:** open — phase B (root-cause fix) verified correctly implemented and code-scoped as a single-variable change; this does NOT close the BUG. The mandatory clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-run (item (c)) plus the secondary risk-lexicon/broader-taxonomy audit on that re-run (ADR-015 condition 2/3) have not been executed and remain the sole blocker to closure.
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (re-gate triggered by HANDOFF: code changed after the W3a GATE:PASS)

**0. Diagnostic run confirms the hypothesis, independently re-checked.** Read `docs/ai/simulation_results/VP-003/VP-003_20260709_110457_domain_inference.json` (untracked artifact, mode=`rag`) directly: `finish_reason == "length"`, `usage == {"prompt_tokens": 2665, "completion_tokens": 1536, "total_tokens": 4201}` — `completion_tokens` exactly equals the pre-fix `max_tokens` ceiling, confirming truncation, not natural completion. Read `_call` (lines 136-171) in full: the only functional change from the phase-A state is the literal integer argument to `chat_timed(..., max_tokens=...)`; the `_call`/`run()` plumbing (5-tuple return, logging, schema persistence) is unchanged from phase A.

**2. No other retrieval/prompt/chunk change slipped in.** `git diff --stat HEAD` (repo root, `9d857fe`) shows the same 10-file set as the phase-A gate: `domain_inference.py`, `f2_grounding.py`, `f2.py`, `schemas/domain_inference.py`, `test_bug_016.py`, `test_bug_017.py`, `test_domain_inference.py`, `test_f2_grounding.py`, `test_f2_pipeline.py`, `v2.system.md` — no new file introduced. `apps/ai-server/src/rag/retrieval.py` shows **zero diff** (`git diff --stat HEAD -- apps/ai-server/src/rag/`, empty output) — no chunk trimming/rerank/cap (Track-3 option #2, REV-011 confound concern) touched anything. `f2.py`'s diff (`_STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS = ("chief_complaint", "history_of_present_illness")`) and `f2_grounding.py`'s diff (`_normalize_rag_chunk_source_id`) are byte-identical to what phase A/BUG-016 already shipped — re-confirmed by direct grep, no additional edits since. `docs/ai/prompts/domain_inference/v2.system.md`'s diff is the single BUG-016 defense-in-depth sentence only (`source_id는 대괄호 안 값을 접두사 없이 그대로 복사한다`) — unchanged since phase A, nothing new for phase B.

**3. Diff isolated to exactly 2 files this dispatch (mtime evidence).** `stat` on all 10 changed files: 8 cluster at 10:37:09–10:43:49 KST 2026-07-09 (`f2_grounding.py`, `schemas/domain_inference.py`, `f2.py`, `v2.system.md`, `test_domain_inference.py`, `test_f2_grounding.py`, `test_f2_pipeline.py`, `test_bug_016.py` — the Wave-2/phase-A + BUG-016 + VAL-010 session, untouched since); `domain_inference.py` (11:09:25) and `test_bug_017.py` (11:10:12) form a distinct, later 47-second cluster — this dispatch's edit session. Matches HANDOFF's claim exactly.

Earlier fixes independently confirmed still intact on the branch.** `grep -n _normalize_rag_chunk_source_id src/eval/f2_grounding.py` → present (`f2_grounding.py:207`, called at `:280` inside the `rag_chunk` branch) — BUG-016 normalization intact. `run()`'s finish_reason/usage capture-and-log-on-every-outcome path (phase A) unchanged, re-confirmed by test 4 above passing without modification. `grep -n _STAGE1_QUERY_SLOTS src/f2.py` → `("chief_complaint", "history_of_present_illness")` (line 75), no `risk_assessment` — VAL-010 mitigation intact.

**GATE: PASS.** The BUG-017 phase B delta is a genuine single-variable change (`max_tokens` 1536→4096, global/unconditional, in `domain_inference.py`'s `_call`) plus its corresponding test-assertion update in `test_bug_017.py` — no retrieval, chunk-selection, or prompt-content change is bundled with it (REV-011's binding condition holds). The full remediation set on `feat/f2-rag-remediation` (BUG-016 resolved, BUG-017 phase A+B code-verified, VAL-010 code mitigation intact) is green at 707/707, ruff/mypy clean, diff-scope confirmed to exactly the 2 claimed files for this delta (10 files total across the whole branch). **This re-clears the code for the certification batch.** BUG-017 stays **open**: closure still requires (i) the clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-run (this diagnostic run does not count — it is a single confirmatory call, not the n=2 clean pair REV-010 resolution #1 specifies) and (ii) that re-run passing the secondary manual risk-lexicon/broader-taxonomy audit (ADR-015 condition 2/3, REV-011 §3 rule i/ii — the `retrieval_meta.queries` audit must also cover `chief_complaint`/HPI content per REV-011's binding extension, not merely `risk_assessment`'s absence).

### Closure verification | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Status:** resolved
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (per critic REV-015 Part A §A.5/§A.7, "BUG-017 may all close" authorization)

Both of this entry's own stated closure conditions (phase B gate, immediately above) are now independently confirmed satisfied, checked directly against the EXP-008 artifacts (not accepted from REV-015's or `result.md`'s prose):

1. **Condition (i) — clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-run.** `experiments/EXP-008/runs/VP-003/run{1,2}/VP-003/*_domain_inference.json`, read directly this gate: both `finish_reason == "stop"` (not `"length"` — no truncation recurrence), `validation_errors is None`, `raw_response is None` — both runs parsed and schema-validated cleanly. `completion_tokens` = 2,028 (run1) and 606 (run2), both well under the 4,096 ceiling BUG-017 phase B set — the diagnosed truncation mechanism (`max_tokens=1536` under-provisioning) is confirmed not recurring. 0/4 `finish_reason="length"` across the full EXP-008 batch (VP-001 included).
2. **Condition (ii) — secondary risk-lexicon/broader-taxonomy audit passing on that re-run.** `result.md` EXP-008 "Secondary taxonomy audit" table (independently cross-checked against the raw `whitelist_audit.counts.accepted` values in the same 4 artifact files this gate re-read: 11+0+18+13 = 42, matching): 0/42 accepted evidence quotes match the broader taxonomy. Rollback trigger does not fire.

**Distinction from BUG-019 (also resolved this pass, separate entry below) — stated explicitly per REV-015 §A.5's requirement so a future reader does not conflate the two:** BUG-017 is the **truncation mechanism** (`max_tokens=1536` too low for RAG-mode's longer prompt, causing the LLM's own output to be cut off mid-generation — signature: `finish_reason="length"`, `completion_tokens` pinned at the ceiling). It was diagnosed and fixed in phase B (`max_tokens` raised to 4096) and its non-recurrence is directly re-confirmed above (`finish_reason="stop"` on all 4 EXP-008 runs, well under the new ceiling). BUG-019, by contrast, is a **different failure mode that surfaced only after BUG-017's fix landed** — the LLM completed generation normally (`finish_reason="stop"`) but produced a `source_type` field-value collision that failed Pydantic schema validation; BUG-017's fix does not touch that code path at all. The two are independently diagnosed, independently fixed, and independently confirmed non-recurring this batch — this closure note does not merge their evidence or their fixes.

**Standing condition (does not reopen BUG-017):** BUG-017's diagnosed mechanism (`max_tokens` truncation) is closed for good — it is a fixed, deterministic ceiling change, not a probabilistic behavior requiring ongoing monitoring. The continuing standing checks (`finish_reason`/`usage`/`raw_response`/`validation_errors` reporting on every future RAG batch, per REV-015 §A.6/§A.7) exist primarily as BUG-019's regression check, not BUG-017's — but since the same instrumentation also trivially re-confirms BUG-017's non-recurrence each time (`finish_reason != "length"`), no separate standing check is needed for BUG-017 specifically.

**Linked:** REV-015 (`discussion.md`, Part A §A.5/§A.7), EXP-008 (`result.md`), BUG-019 (distinct mechanism, also resolved)
