## [BUG-018] `agent_model_registry.yaml` misdeclares `temporal_summary`/`evidence_verifier` as LLM-routed | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Severity:** minor
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/routing/agent_model_registry.yaml:122-138` (`temporal_summary`), `:158-174` (`evidence_verifier`)
**Linked:** PLAN-2026-W28-F (Stage 3 qa gate), `docs/ai/agents/09_temporal_summary.md`, `docs/ai/agents/11_evidence_verifier.md` (both spec files now correctly disclose the as-built rule-based reality and flag this exact drift as "알려진 드리프트, 별도 BUG 예정")
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | `cat apps/ai-server/src/routing/agent_model_registry.yaml` — `temporal_summary` (lines 122-138) and `evidence_verifier` (lines 158-174) both declare `strategy: benchmarked` with a full primary(`solar-pro3`)/secondary(`k-exaone`)/fallback(`ak-llm`) tier structure, identical in shape to genuinely LLM-routed agents (e.g. `safety_classifier`, `clinical_slot`). |
| 2 | `grep -n "router\|adapter\|prompt_loader\|PromptLoader\|ModelRouter" apps/ai-server/src/agents/temporal_summary.py` → zero matches |
| 3 | `grep -n "router\|adapter\|prompt_loader\|PromptLoader\|ModelRouter" apps/ai-server/src/agents/evidence_verifier.py` → zero matches |
| 4 | Read `apps/ai-server/src/agents/temporal_summary.py:1,34-35` — module docstring says "rule-based longitudinal state comparison"; `TemporalSummaryAgent.run()` hardcodes `model_used="rule-engine"`, `prompt_version="v1"` (a cosmetic label — no prompt is ever loaded) |
| 5 | Read `apps/ai-server/src/agents/evidence_verifier.py:161,219,234,255,302,339` — exactly 6 `_check_*` methods, all regex/pattern-matching, no LLM call site anywhere in the file |
| 6 | Read `apps/ai-server/src/routing/model_router.py:70-90` (`ModelRouter.select_model`) — the registry entry is only consulted when an agent's own code calls `self._router.select_model(self.agent_name, ...)`; neither `temporal_summary.py` nor `evidence_verifier.py` contains any such call, so the wrong entries are currently inert at runtime (no live mis-routing), but the registry file itself remains a false machine-readable description of these two agents' architecture |

**Expected:** `agent_model_registry.yaml` accurately reflects each agent's actual routing behavior — either `strategy: fixed, adapter: none` (mirroring the `orchestrator` entry's own `rule-based` pattern, `agent_model_registry.yaml:98-102`) or a dedicated `strategy: offline`/`rule_based` value, for any agent that never calls `ModelRouter.select_model()`.
**Actual:** Both `temporal_summary` and `evidence_verifier` are declared `strategy: benchmarked` with 3-tier LLM adapter/model configuration, identical in shape to genuinely LLM-routed agents, despite neither agent's code ever consulting the router. A reader of the registry alone (a benchmarking dashboard, cost-attribution script, or engineer onboarding from config rather than code) would incorrectly conclude these two agents call `solar-pro3`/`k-exaone`/`ak-llm` at runtime.

**Minimal repro:** none written (docs-only mission, `tests/` out of scope for this gate per PLAN-2026-W28-F step 3 charter — repro is the grep/read commands above, independently re-runnable).

**Proposed fix (not prescribed):** change both entries' `strategy` to `fixed`/`adapter: none` (or a new `rule_based` strategy value if `ModelRouter` should distinguish "fixed non-LLM" from "rule-based, registry-exempt"), mirroring the `orchestrator` entry. Out of scope for this docs-only mission (PLAN-2026-W28-F step 3 charter: registry is code, not `docs/ai/agents/`) — flagged for a future `developer` pass.

## [BUG-019] VP-003 RAG-mode schema-validation-failure — root cause undiagnosable, raw LLM response not persisted | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/agents/domain_inference.py:124-134` (`_parse`), `:264-277` (`run`, schema-validation-failure degrade path); `apps/ai-server/src/schemas/domain_inference.py` (`DomainInferenceOutput` — no raw-response field)
**Affected EXP:** EXP-006 (`result.md`) RAG arm — `rag/VP-003/run1`, `rag/VP-003/run2` (both, still 0/2 interpretable after BUG-017 phase B) and `rag/VP-001/run1` (new failure, previously clean in EXP-005). ADR-015's RAG-arm certification condition (2) — "a clean VP-003 RAG n=2 re-verification" — is unmet by this batch for a reason distinct from the one it was gated on.
**Linked:** BUG-017 (**resolved for its own diagnosed mechanism — truncation; this entry does NOT reopen BUG-017**, see Distinction below), REV-010, REV-011 §3 rule iv, ADR-015, EXP-006 (`result.md`, Key finding #4/#5, Notes' explicit "recommend qa file a new BUG" pointer)
**Status:** open

### Reproduction

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | Artifact evidence (no live LLM re-run performed by qa — none available/in charter), independently re-read from the raw JSON, not accepted from `result.md`'s prose: `experiments/EXP-006/runs/rag/VP-003/run1/VP-003_20260709_112154_domain_inference.json`, `.../run2/VP-003_20260709_112208_domain_inference.json`, `experiments/EXP-006/runs/rag/VP-001/run1/VP-001_20260709_112116_domain_inference.json` |
| 2 | Corresponding `stdout.log` lines, independently re-read verbatim (all three quoted below) |
| 3 | Code-path repro (no live LLM): `cd apps/ai-server && source .venv/bin/activate && python -m pytest tests/repro/test_bug_019.py -v` |

**Actual (artifact evidence, verbatim, independently re-confirmed by qa via direct JSON read — not copied from `result.md`):**
- `rag/VP-003/run1/stdout.log`: `"DomainInference parse failure: LLM response schema validation failure: 1 error(s) (model=solar-pro3-260323, finish_reason=stop, usage={'prompt_tokens': 2665, 'completion_tokens': 727, 'total_tokens': 3392})"`
- `rag/VP-003/run2/stdout.log`: `"... schema validation failure: 3 error(s) ... finish_reason=stop, usage={'prompt_tokens': 2273, 'completion_tokens': 854, 'total_tokens': 3127}"`
- `rag/VP-001/run1/stdout.log` (new this batch — VP-001 was clean in both of its EXP-005 RAG runs): `"... schema validation failure: 3 error(s) ... finish_reason=stop, usage={'prompt_tokens': 3582, 'completion_tokens': 1590, 'total_tokens': 5172}"`

All three artifact JSONs independently confirmed (`python -c "import json; json.load(open(...))"`, this gate): `filter_summary.candidates_before=0`, `domain_candidates=[]`, `finish_reason="stop"` (all 3), and no key resembling `raw_content`/`raw_response`/`llm_response` anywhere in the artifact — matching `top-level keys = ['additional_questions', 'chunk_texts', 'department_candidates', 'domain_candidates', 'filter_summary', 'finish_reason', 'latency_ms', 'model_used', 'orphan_departments', 'persona_id', 'prompt_version', 'repro', 'retrieval_meta', 'session_id', 'summary', 'usage', 'utterances', 'whitelist_audit']` for all three files.

### Distinction from BUG-017 (resolved, does NOT reopen)

BUG-017 diagnosed and fixed a specific, falsified-and-confirmed mechanism: `max_tokens=1536` truncation, signature `finish_reason="length"` + `completion_tokens` exactly at the ceiling (confirmed live by the W4a diagnostic run, `experiments/EXP-006/diagnostic/`). BUG-017 phase B (`max_tokens` 1536→4096, code-verified single-variable change, qa GATE:PASS 2026-07-09) closed that mechanism — and it stayed closed this batch: all 3 of this entry's failures show `finish_reason="stop"` (natural completion, not truncation) with `completion_tokens` (727/854/1590) far below the new 4096 ceiling. **This is a different failure mode reached the same practical outcome (0 interpretable VP-003 RAG results) via a different mechanism** — the LLM completed generation normally but produced syntactically-valid JSON that fails Pydantic schema validation. `tests/repro/test_bug_019.py::TestBug019FinishReasonProvesNotBug017` demonstrates the two modes are distinguishable precisely because BUG-017 phase A's own `finish_reason`/`usage` instrumentation now exists — without it, this new failure would be indistinguishable from a second instance of BUG-017's mechanism from the logs alone.

### Root cause: UNKNOWN — not diagnosable from current logs/artifacts

**Why undiagnosable.** `DomainInferenceAgent._parse` (`domain_inference.py:124-134`) is:
```python
try:
    return DomainInferenceLLMResponse.model_validate(data), ""
except ValidationError as exc:
    return None, f"LLM response schema validation failure: {exc.error_count()} error(s)"
```
This returns only `exc.error_count()` — a bare integer — never `exc.errors()` (the field-level detail: which field(s) failed, what value was received, which constraint was violated) and never the `content`/`data` that was being validated. `run()` (`:264-277`) threads this count-only `reason` string into `DomainInferenceOutput.reason_summary`, logs it (`model_used`, `finish_reason`, `usage` alongside it — all confirmed present in the log lines above), but has no code path that captures or persists the raw LLM response text anywhere: not on `DomainInferenceOutput` (`src/schemas/domain_inference.py` — the only BUG-017-phase-A additions are `finish_reason: str | None` and `usage: dict[str, int] | None`, both confirmed by direct read to carry no response-body content), not in a log line, and not in `apps/ai-server/src/f2.py`'s artifact/report builders (`_repro_metadata`/`_build_artifact`, confirmed by direct read — both forward only `finish_reason`/`usage`, matching what actually landed in all 3 real artifacts above).

**Practical consequence:** it is not knowable from this project's own logs/artifacts whether these 3 failures share one root cause (e.g. a specific field the model consistently gets wrong under RAG-mode prompt content) or three unrelated ones, whether `_RISK_PHRASES`/risk-lexicon-cascade interaction plays any role (the cascade never ran — `candidates_before=0` — so this can be ruled out as *this* run's cause, but not ruled out as an upstream influence on why the model produced invalid output), or which specific `DomainCandidate`/`DepartmentCandidate` field(s) the model got wrong. `exc.error_count()` alone (1, 3, 3) tells us only that something failed and how many things — not what.

**Correlation carried forward from REV-010/REV-011/VAL-010 (not re-derived here, cited):** VP-003's RAG-mode retrieval remains the most risk-topic-saturated in the batch (REV-011 §2, `result.md` EXP-006 "Queries audit" section) — a plausible contributor to harder-to-format model output, but explicitly not established as causal by either critic or the tracker, and this entry does not upgrade that framing. VP-001/run1's new failure is not explained by the same query-saturation pattern (VP-001's queries are reported benign, `result.md` EXP-006 "Queries audit" table) — this is itself informative: whatever produces the schema-invalid output is not exclusively a VP-003-risk-content phenomenon, which is one more reason the raw-response gap needs closing before a root cause can be assigned with confidence.

### Minimal repro

`apps/ai-server/tests/repro/test_bug_019.py` (new, qa, no live LLM, no `src/` changes — offline-testable per this gate's charter):
- `TestBug019FinishReasonProvesNotBug017` (1 test) — confirms a schema-validation failure with `finish_reason="stop"` is mechanically distinguishable from BUG-017's truncation signature, demonstrating why this is filed as a new BUG rather than a BUG-017 reopening.
- `TestBug019RawResponseNotPersistedAnywhere` (2 tests) — the core diagnostic-gap demonstration: (a) `DomainInferenceOutput.model_dump()` carries `finish_reason`/`usage` (BUG-017 phase A, confirmed still working) but no field containing `raw`/`content`/`response` in its name, and `reason_summary` carries only the bare error count; (b) a direct unit check on `DomainInferenceAgent._parse` itself confirms the richer `exc.errors()` detail (5 field-level entries, for this fixture) is available at the exact point `_parse` discards it down to `exc.error_count()`.
- `TestBug019JsonParseFailurePathAlsoDiscardsContent` (1 test) — confirms the sibling `JSONDecodeError` branch of `_parse` has the identical discard property, i.e. this is a `_parse`-wide gap, not specific to the `ValidationError` branch.

4/4 tests pass, demonstrating the current (buggy/gap) state, per this project's established repro-test convention (BUG-007/009/014/015/016/017 precedent — pass now, invert on fix). Full suite: `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **711 passed, 0 failed** (707 pre-existing + 4 new, all in `test_bug_019.py`). `ruff check tests/repro/test_bug_019.py` → all checks passed. `mypy tests/repro/test_bug_019.py` → 11 errors, all `attr-defined`/`method-assign` on `MagicMock(...).return_value =`-style router/adapter mocking — independently confirmed to be the identical pre-existing false-positive pattern already present in `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py` (10 errors, same shape, same lines-of-reasoning), not a defect introduced by this file; consistent with BUG-016's QA note that this class of mypy warning is pre-existing and repo-wide on this test-mocking convention.

### Recommended diagnostic (not prescribed — instrument first, then diagnose, then fix, mirroring BUG-017 phase A's own precedent)

1. **Persist the raw LLM response text on schema-validation failure** (and, for symmetry/cheapness, on JSON-parse failure too — same `_parse`-wide gap, `TestBug019JsonParseFailurePathAlsoDiscardsContent` above) — e.g. add a `raw_content: str | None` field to `DomainInferenceOutput`, threaded through `run()`'s degrade-path construction the same way `finish_reason`/`usage` already are, and mirrored into `f2.py`'s `_repro_metadata`/`_build_artifact` (or a sibling field) so it lands in the saved artifact JSON, not just an in-process value. Consider also logging/persisting `exc.errors()` (the field-level detail), not just `exc.error_count()`, at the same touch point — cheap, and directly answers "which field, what value, what constraint" without needing the raw text at all for the schema-shape half of the question. Data-sensitivity note: the raw content is model output, not patient PII by construction (F2's own input redaction/architecture is unrelated to this field) — no new data-handling review is anticipated, but flagged for developer to confirm during implementation.
2. **Then diagnose**, using the newly-persisted data, on a fresh VP-003 (and VP-001) RAG re-run: read the actual invalid JSON and `exc.errors()` output to determine the specific field(s)/pattern the model gets wrong under RAG-mode prompt content — candidate hypotheses to check (not prescribed, not yet evidenced): a `domain`/`department` enum-value drift, a `confidence` range violation, an `evidence` array shape issue analogous to BUG-016's `chunk_id=` prefix-echo class, or prompt-length/retrieved-chunk-content interaction (per the REV-011 correlation note above).
3. **Then fix**, whichever the diagnostic data points to — out of qa's charter (developer's job); this entry does not prescribe a fix.
4. Re-run VP-003 RAG n=2 (and, given VP-001's new failure, consider VP-001 RAG n=2 as well) cleanly, per ADR-015 condition 2/3, before any RAG-arm claim about either persona is made.

**Not in scope of this entry:** fixing the failure (developer), the RAG-arm certification verdict (critic, W5), running any further live experiments (experiment-tracker).

### Wave-3 instrumentation verification (Track M) + code gate — qa | 2026-07-09

**Status:** open — instrumentation (the recommended diagnostic step 1 above) verified correctly implemented and genuinely non-vacuous; this does NOT close the BUG. Root cause is still unknown, no root-cause fix has been attempted this wave, and no live VP-003/VP-001 RAG re-run has been performed (out of this gate's charter — no live LLM/DB experiment, `experiment_gate` respected). Stays **open**, per this project's own BUG-017-phase-A precedent for an instrumentation-only delta.
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (PLAN-2026-W28-H Wave 4 gate, full-branch: BUG-019 instrumentation + Track A route retirement + Track B AI-disease container/HPI-isolation + Track C `continuous_test.py`, all on `feat/f2-continuation-w28h`, no commits yet)

**1. `_parse` now returns `(parsed, reason, validation_errors)`, and both failure branches persist `raw_response`.** Read `src/agents/domain_inference.py:124-158` in full: the `JSONDecodeError` branch returns `(None, reason, None)` (no `ValidationError` was ever raised, so `validation_errors` is correctly `None`); the `ValidationError` branch returns `(None, reason, [dict(e) for e in exc.errors()])`. `run()` (`:280-310`) threads both, plus the already-in-scope `raw_content`, into `DomainInferenceOutput.raw_response`/`.validation_errors` on **both** failure branches — confirmed by direct read, not by trusting the shipped test alone.

**2. Independent, non-shipped-fixture mutation/confirmation (this gate, not `test_bug_019.py`'s own fixtures).** Two fresh, independently-constructed payloads (not copied from the test file) run directly through the real code path:
   - `DomainInferenceAgent._parse()` called directly on a fresh invalid-domain/invalid-confidence payload → `validation_errors` is a 3-entry list of real Pydantic error dicts (`type`/`loc`/`msg`/`input`/`ctx`/`url` keys), not a placeholder.
   - `DomainInferenceAgent.run()` called end-to-end (mocked adapter, independently-constructed fixture `'{"domain_candidates": [{"domain": "anxiety", "confidence": 5.0, "evidence": []}]}'`) → `out.raw_response == <the exact fixture text>` and `out.validation_errors` is a genuine 2-entry Pydantic error-detail list (confidence range + empty-evidence violations), confirming the plumbing is real end-to-end, not hardcoded or test-fixture-specific.

**3. `f2.py` artifact/report plumbing confirmed by direct read.** `_repro_metadata` (`f2.py:249-283`) gains `raw_response`/`validation_errors` kwargs written into the `repro` dict; `_build_artifact` (`:408-411`) mirrors both at the top level; `_build_report` (`:540-556`) renders a new "## LLM failure diagnostics (BUG-019)" section **only when `artifact.get("raw_response") is not None`** — i.e. it is additive and does not alter the report for the (common) clean-success case. `_run()`'s call site (`:647-649`) passes `output.raw_response`/`output.validation_errors` through — traced end-to-end from `DomainInferenceOutput` to the saved artifact JSON and the markdown report, no dropped hop.

**4. Suite, lint, type — independently re-run, not accepted from HANDOFF's claim.** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **749 passed, 0 failed** (before qa's own new `tests/test_hpi_isolation.py`; 758 passed after — see Track B verification below). `ruff check` on all 9 modified/new src files in this wave (`domain_inference.py`, `f2.py`, `main.py`, `rag/auth.py`, `rag/route.py`, `rag_chat.py`, `schemas/domain_inference.py`, `schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`, `continuous_test.py`) → all checks passed; full-repo `ruff check src/ tests/` → all checks passed. `mypy` on the same 9 source files → no issues found. `mypy` on the 4 new/modified test files → the only errors are the identical pre-existing `attr-defined`/`method-assign` false-positive pattern on `MagicMock(...).return_value =`-style router/adapter mocking already baselined at `tests/repro/test_bug_017.py` (confirmed by diffing error counts against a clean `git stash` baseline: 11 pre-existing errors across `test_bug_017.py`+`test_route_auth.py` before this branch's changes, matching this branch's post-change count on the same two files) — no new mypy defect class introduced.

**5. Diff-scope confirmed.** `git status --porcelain` (repo root) shows exactly: the 9 modified files above, plus 4 untracked developer files (`continuous_test.py`, `schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/test_ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/test_continuous_test.py`) and qa's own new `tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` — no PRD/checklist/agent-spec/`.env.example` files touched (Track D/writer work correctly not yet done this wave; out of this gate's scope).

**GATE (instrumentation dimension): PASS.** Mirrors BUG-017 phase A's own disposition exactly: the diagnostic gap this entry originally identified (raw LLM response text + field-level `ValidationError.errors()` detail both discarded, undiagnosable from logs/artifacts) is now closed for **future** runs. It does **not** retroactively diagnose the 3 already-failed EXP-006 runs (their artifacts predate this instrumentation and were not re-run — no live LLM access in this gate, per charter) and does not itself establish a root cause. **BUG-019 stays open** pending: (a) the live diagnostic capture (experiment-tracker, Wave 5, `PLAN-2026-W28-H` §順序(a)) using this now-verified instrumentation, (b) a root-cause fix once that capture is read, and (c) the clean VP-003(+VP-001) RAG n=2 re-verification (ADR-016's certification path).

### Track A (RAG HTTP retirement) — qa live verification | 2026-07-09

Independently re-confirmed (not accepted from developer's docstring claims): `TestClient(src.main.app).post("/ai/rag/grounding", ...)` → **404** (route absent — never 503/401/403, which would imply mounted-and-authed; never 200, which would imply an unauthed leak); `TestClient(src.main.app).get("/health")` → 200 (other routes unaffected); `[r.path for r in app.routes if "rag" in r.path]` → `[]` (zero rag-prefixed paths mounted in the running app at all). Matches REV-013 §2's gate intent ("no live-mounted, network-reachable RAG HTTP surface") exactly. `src/rag/route.py`/`src/rag/auth.py` confirmed retired-not-deleted (present in tree, header comments cite ADR-017/REV-013, `tests/rag/test_route_auth.py` converted to mount the router into a standalone throwaway app rather than `src.main.app`, plus a new `TestRagRouteRetiredFromMainApp` class asserting the 404 above) — matches the developer's own stated scope-note choice in REV-013 §2. `UPSTAGE_API_KEY` grep confirms it remains structurally disjoint from `NS_RAG_API_KEY` (unaffected either way, as required).

### Track C (`continuous_test.py`) — DB password handling verification | 2026-07-09

`_mask_dsn()` (`src/continuous_test.py:65-74`) read in full: replaces the password component of a parsed DSN with `***` via `urlsplit`/`urlunsplit`, degrades to a fixed `"***unparseable-dsn***"` sentinel on any parse exception (never re-raises, never falls through to an unmasked value). Independently tested (not just the shipped `TestMaskDsnNeverLeaksPassword` suite): a live `create_async_engine`+`async_sessionmaker` connection attempt against an unreachable host with an embedded test password (`postgresql+asyncpg://neurosync:S3CR3T_PW_TEST@127.0.0.1:1/neurosync`) raises `OSError: [Errno 111] Connect call failed (...)` — confirmed the password string does **not** appear anywhere in the raised exception's `str()`, so `_db_preflight`'s `f"UNREACHABLE ({masked}): {exc}"` construction (which does interpolate the raw exception object) cannot leak the password via that path either. `get_settings().database_url` (`src/config.py:77-80`) has a non-empty default (`postgresql+asyncpg://neurosync:dev@localhost:5432/neurosync`, a known dev-only placeholder consistent with this codebase's existing convention, not a new secret) — so `_db_preflight`'s unguarded `get_settings().database_url` call (outside its own `try` block) cannot raise a missing-env-var exception that would bypass masking. `grep -n "database_url\|print(\|logger\."` across the whole file confirms the only two `print()` call sites that render stage detail strings source their content from already-masked `StageResult.detail` strings, never a raw `Settings` object. **No password-leak path found — verified, not merely trusted.**

### Track B (AI-disease container / HPI isolation) — qa adversarial test suite | 2026-07-09

New file `apps/ai-server/tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` (qa-owned, per REV-013 §3 binding condition 1) — 9 tests across 3 classes, one per REV-013 §3 binding-condition item:

| Class | Tests | Covers |
|:--|:--|:--|
| `TestHPIIsolationChannelExtra` | 3 | REV-013 §3(1a): `HandoffInput(..., extra={"ai_predicted_disease": [...]})` → `_build_user_content` output never contains the marker (also generalized to an arbitrary `extra` payload, not just the literal key name) |
| `TestHPIIsolationChannelConversationHistoryCallGraph` | 4 | REV-013 §3(1b): static grep confirming `dialogue.py`/`orchestrator.py` never reference the AI-disease module/class names, plus a behavioral check on both concrete `conversation_history.append` call sites confirming pure passthrough of caller-supplied text |
| `TestHPIIsolationLiveBehaviorContainer` | 2 | brainstorm §2(c)/REV-013 §3: injects a real `AIPredictedDiseaseOutput` (via `state.slot_data` and via `HandoffInput.extra`) and runs the REAL `HandoffGeneratorAgent.run()` code path against an **echo-wired** mocked adapter (the adapter returns exactly the prompt text it received as `report_markdown` — chosen specifically so a genuine prompt-layer leak would surface in the output, unlike a canned-response mock) — asserts the marker disease name/similarity-score never reach `report_markdown`, with a non-vacuity sanity check that a real (non-leaked) slot value DOES flow through end-to-end |

Full suite with this file included: **758 passed, 0 failed**. `ruff check tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` → all checks passed. `mypy tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` → 4 errors, all the same pre-existing `attr-defined`/`method-assign` mocking false-positive pattern baselined above — no new defect class.

**Mutation/non-vacuity checks (this gate, in-memory only, not part of the shipped file):** (a) for the `extra` channel, monkeypatched `_build_user_content` to simulate a future regression (append `inp.extra` to the prompt when present) and confirmed the corresponding assertion would fail — the test is genuinely load-bearing, not vacuously true. (b) for the live-behavior container check, monkeypatched `handoff_generator._build_user_content` to simulate the same regression and re-ran the full injected-`AIPredictedDiseaseOutput` → `HandoffGeneratorAgent.run()` → `report_markdown` path end-to-end: the marker leaked into `report_markdown` as expected under the mutation, and the test's assertion correctly caught it. Both mutations reverted (in-memory monkeypatch only, no source file touched).

**Independent re-confirmation of REV-013 §4 labeling conditions (not re-derived from developer's own `test_ai_predicted_disease.py`, checked directly against the schema):** `AIPredictedDiseaseOutput.model_fields["is_diagnostic"].annotation` → `typing.Literal[False]` (the type itself, not merely the runtime default) — confirmed `is_diagnostic=True` raises `ValidationError` on construction. `grep -n "probability\|확률\|confidence\|softmax"` across `schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py` and `f2.py` → the only hits are docstring/description prose explicitly prohibiting those labels, or `DomainCandidate.confidence` (a pre-existing, disjoint F2 field, never referenced by the AI-disease schema) — no `probability`/`confidence`/softmax field or computation exists on the AI-disease entity itself.

**Open item (non-blocking, flagged for orchestrator/critic awareness, not filed as a BUG):** the brainstorm design note's §2 layer-1 type invariant (`model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")` on `SlotData`/`HandoffInput`/`ClinicalSlotOutput`) was not implemented this wave — `grep -rn "extra=\"forbid\"|ConfigDict" src/schemas/handoff.py src/schemas/clinical_slot.py` returns no hits. This is not a functional gap for the two channels REV-013 §3 actually named (REV-013 §3 itself notes `extra="forbid"` has no effect on the already-declared `AgentInput.extra` field, so it would not have closed channel (a) either way) — all 9 isolation tests above pass without it, live-verified with mutation checks. It is a completeness gap against the design note's own stated Wave-3 scope ("build ... the type-layer/call-graph-layer invariants (§2.1–2)") worth a developer follow-up for defense-in-depth against unrelated future accidental-kwarg bugs, not a leak risk today.

**GATE (Track A/B/C, this wave): PASS.**

### Closure verification (live re-verification, ADR-016 condition 2) | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Status:** resolved
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (per critic REV-015 Part A §A.5/§A.7, "BUG-019 may CLOSE — qa to perform the formal tracker updates" authorization)

The single remaining blocker from the Wave-6 gate above — a live clean VP-003 + VP-001 RAG n=2 re-verification — is now discharged. Independently re-checked directly against the raw EXP-008 artifact JSONs this gate (not accepted from REV-015's or `result.md`'s prose):

```
experiments/EXP-008/runs/VP-001/run1/.../*_domain_inference.json: finish_reason=stop validation_errors=None raw_response=None mode=rag
experiments/EXP-008/runs/VP-001/run2/.../*_domain_inference.json: finish_reason=stop validation_errors=None raw_response=None mode=rag
experiments/EXP-008/runs/VP-003/run1/.../*_domain_inference.json: finish_reason=stop validation_errors=None raw_response=None mode=rag
experiments/EXP-008/runs/VP-003/run2/.../*_domain_inference.json: finish_reason=stop validation_errors=None raw_response=None mode=rag
```

**Criterion verified: VP-003 n=2 + VP-001 n=2 all interpretable, `validation_errors`/`raw_response` both `null` on all 4 — no `source_type` table-origin collision recurred.** `_parse()` never entered its failure branch on any of the 4 genuinely-new live calls this batch (fresh `generated_at` timestamps and matching `httpx` POST lines in each run's `stdout.log`, per `result.md` EXP-008 §"Input reproducibility" — not re-derived here, cited). VP-003/run2's 0-candidate outcome (`filter_summary.candidates_before=1, candidates_after=0`, all 3 evidence items independently confirmed `rejected_risk_lexicon` on genuine risk-lexicon anchors in the cited `case_card` text) is a legitimate cascade elimination, not a parse/schema failure — `_parse()` still returned non-`None` for that run, satisfying the interpretability bar cleanly. This is the first batch in this project's history (EXP-004 through EXP-007 all showed at least one VP-003 RAG failure) where every VP-003 and VP-001 RAG-mode call cleared `_parse()`.

ADR-016 condition (2) is met. BUG-019's root cause (`evidence.source_type` DB-table-origin collision, "case_card"/"qa" intermittently echoed into the field instead of the correct literal) — diagnosed via EXP-007, fixed via `_normalize_source_type_collision` (narrow regex `^(?:case_card|qa)(?::\d+)?$`, pre-validation placement, code-verified with mutation testing, qa GATE:PASS Wave 6, suite 768) — is now confirmed non-recurring on a live, genuinely-new-calls batch.

**Distinction from BUG-017 (also resolved, separate entry above):** BUG-019 is the `source_type`-field-value-collision mechanism, mechanically distinct from BUG-017's truncation mechanism — see the Distinction note under BUG-017's own closure entry for the full statement. Do not attribute this closure's evidence to BUG-017 or vice versa; the two were diagnosed, fixed, and re-verified independently, on different code paths (`_parse`'s pre-validation coercion vs. `_call`'s `max_tokens` ceiling).

**Standing conditions — these do NOT reopen BUG-019, and remain mandatory going forward (REV-015 Part A §A.6/§A.7, restated here for this tracker entry's own record):**
- The secondary taxonomy audit and the `retrieval_meta.queries` audit (covering `chief_complaint`/HPI content, not merely `risk_assessment`'s absence) remain mandatory on every future RAG-mode batch, unconditionally — not a one-time gate that expired on this closure.
- `finish_reason`/`usage`/`raw_response`/`validation_errors` continue to be captured and reported verbatim on every future RAG batch as the standing BUG-019-regression check — this instrumentation is what made this closure possible and is what would catch a recurrence.
- **A future `source_type`-collision-shaped schema-validation failure reopens BUG-019 — it is not filed as a new BUG.** ("Collision-shaped" means: `finish_reason="stop"` (not truncation) + a schema-validation error whose failing field/value pattern matches `evidence.source_type` receiving a DB-table-origin literal such as `"case_card"`/`"qa"` or a `:<digits>`-suffixed variant, rather than the correct `"rag_chunk"`/`"utterance"` literal.)
- This closure rests on a single confirmatory live batch (n=2 per persona) following a documented history of intermittent, non-deterministic failures on byte-identical inputs (EXP-007: 2/4 reproduced, 2/4 did not, pre-fix) — critic's REV-015 §A.6 assessed this as non-disqualifying given the fix is a deterministic, narrow, mutation-tested code mechanism rather than a statistical/prompt-wording change, but flags it as a standing evidentiary caveat, not a reason to withhold closure. Recorded here so a future reader does not read this closure as resting on an exceptionally large evidence base.

**Linked:** REV-015 (`discussion.md`, Part A §A.5/§A.6/§A.7), EXP-008 (`result.md`), ADR-016, ADR-018 (`discussion.md`, adopts REV-015 Part A), BUG-017 (distinct mechanism, also resolved)

## [VAL-011] AI-predicted-disease live population (path1) has no risk-lexicon filter on candidate evidence quotes | 2026-07-09 | critic

**Target:** PLAN-2026-W28-K Task 3 (not-yet-built); RES-001 §2 (`discussion.md`, population semantics); `apps/ai-server/src/schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`; `apps/ai-server/src/eval/f2_grounding.py` (`_RISK_PHRASES`/`_contains_any`)
**Issue:** RES-001's path1 population design (steps 1-8) derives each `AIPredictedDiseaseCandidate`'s planned `quote` verbatim from a retrieved `case_card`/`qa` chunk's own text, via a deterministic post-processing pass that runs entirely outside `DomainInferenceAgent`'s LLM call and its risk-lexicon whitelist cascade (`f2_grounding.check_evidence`/`_RISK_PHRASES`, ADR-014's code-enforced rule 2). No step in RES-001's design, and no task in PLAN-2026-W28-K, requires this quote to clear that filter before shipping. VP-003 — this project's own governing high-scrutiny persona — is confirmed across 4 consecutive live RAG batches (EXP-005/006/007/008, independently re-verified by REV-010/012/014/015) to retrieve `case_card`/`qa` chunks containing explicit suicide/self-harm content when queried with its own risk-worded `chief_complaint`/HPI slots (VAL-010, open, this file) — `qa:1685` recurs in every one of those batches. Under path1 exactly as specified, a live populated-path run for VP-003 would plausibly ship a container reading, e.g., "AI 예상질환: 우울장애 (similarity_score: 0.8x, 근거: '자살 생각을 표현하며...')" — a named-disease-labeled artifact citing suicide-ideation corpus text as its supporting quote, with zero code-level gate preventing it.
**Why blocking:** This is the same failure class this project already ruled a categorical, no-carve-out rollback trigger for the sibling entity (`domain_candidates`; PLAN-2026-W28-C C-4/ADR-014: "위험 표현이 domain confidence 근거로 사용된 사례") — and a more severe instance of it, because "AI 예상질환" is a named-disease label, closer to a diagnosis than F2's coarser 8-value domain enum, in a project whose own controlling directive for this entity is "AI가 진단하면 안된다" (REV-013 §4). Shipping population without an equivalent filter would not be a wording nuance — it would be a live, code-permitted regression to a defect this project spent three missions (VAL-006→REV-008 blocking rollback→ADR-014→BUG-014/BUG-015→REV-009/REV-010) closing for the neighboring pipeline. Scoped blocking: this blocks only Task 3's disease-evidence-shipping sub-deliverable, not Task 3's #4 code-side half, the k-sweep instrumentation, or Tasks 1/2/4/5's own work.
**Linked review:** REV-016
**Status:** open

### Resolution

| # | What would resolve it |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | Developer applies `_contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES)` (`f2_grounding.py:88-114,216-219`) or an equivalent code-enforced check to every candidate quote before it ships; drops (does not clip/redact) any candidate whose sole matched chunk is risk-lexicon-flagged, mirroring `filter_domain_candidates`'s existing "no evidence, no candidate" `min_length=1` discipline (`f2_grounding.py:370-387`) |
| 2 | qa extends `tests/test_f2_grounding.py` (or an equivalent suite) to cover this new population code path, with adversarial fixtures drawn from the same broader taxonomy already established for `domain_candidates` |
| 3 | Task 6's populated-path EXP entry runs a secondary manual taxonomy audit specifically over `ai_predicted_disease.candidates[].quote` (not only `domain_candidates[].evidence[].quote`), for every VP this mission populates, mirroring the existing ADR-018 standing condition |

### QA code gate | 2026-07-09 | qa

**Status:** resolved — code-side risk-lexicon filter genuinely closed, mutation-checked, non-vacuous; populated-path HPI isolation proven live per REV-016(c) condition 2.
**Verified by:** qa, 2026-07-09 (PLAN-2026-W28-K Task 3/4, mandatory code gate, regulatory red line stage)
**Scope of this pass:** developer's Track B live-population implementation (`src/f2.py`: `_collect_chunk_disease_votes`, `_aggregate_disease_candidates`, `_build_ai_predicted_disease_populated`, `_extract_quote`, `_clamp_similarity_score`, `_build_evidence_provenance_summary`; `src/schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`: optional `source_id`/`quote`; `src/rag/retrieval.py`: `match_diseases_for_chunk_text`). Baseline suite (798) independently re-run before any qa edit, exact match to HANDOFF's claim.

**1. HARD RED LINE — populated-path HPI isolation: PROVEN, not merely asserted.** `apps/ai-server/tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` extended with a new `TestHPIIsolationPopulatedPath` class (3 tests) — distinct from Wave-3's `TestHPIIsolationLiveBehaviorContainer`, which used a synthetic marker (`disease`/`similarity_score` only; `source_id`/`quote` did not exist as fields when those tests were written). The new tests construct a GENUINELY populated `AIPredictedDiseaseCandidate` — real disease name from this project's own 26-disease Ada ontology ("우울 삽화(우울증)"), real `[0,1]`-bounded `similarity_score` (0.83), real `source_id` ("case_card:512"), real verbatim `quote` — and flow it through the REAL `HandoffGeneratorAgent.run()` code path (echo-wired mock adapter, same convention as Wave-3) via BOTH REV-013 §3 channels (`AgentInput.extra`, `state.slot_data`). All four candidate fields (disease/score/source_id/quote) are individually asserted absent from `handoff_input.slots`, the pure `_build_user_content()` output, AND `report_markdown` — the actual clinician-facing artifact. Non-vacuity confirmed structurally (a real, non-leaked `chief_complaint` slot value DOES flow through `report_markdown` in the same run). **Mutation-checked, in-memory only, not shipped in the file** (matches this project's own established Wave-3/BUG-016/BUG-017 gate convention): (a) patched `_build_user_content` to append `inp.extra` when present — re-ran the extra-channel test body directly, the `disease not in report_markdown` assertion correctly raised `AssertionError`, confirming the test is genuinely load-bearing, not vacuous; (b) patched `OrchestratorAgent._build_handoff_input` to merge `state.slot_data["ai_predicted_disease"]` into `slots.risk_factors` — re-ran the slot_data-channel test body directly, the `disease not in slot_values` assertion correctly raised `AssertionError`. Both mutations reverted (context-manager scoped, no source file touched). `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_hpi_isolation.py -v` → **12/12 passed** (9 pre-existing + 3 new).

**2. VAL-011 closure — risk-lexicon filter genuinely closed.** `apps/ai-server/tests/test_f2_grounding.py` extended with `TestVal011AiPredictedDiseasePopulationRiskLexiconGate` (4 tests): a VP-003-style case_card excerpt ("환자는 최근 상담에서 자살 생각을 표현하며 매우 힘들어했다고 진술함.") is confirmed DROPPED — `candidates == []`, not clipped/redacted — from `f2._aggregate_disease_candidates`'s output; the same fixture run through the real async entry point `f2._build_ai_predicted_disease_populated` reduces to a legitimate 0-candidate outcome (`mode="rag_live"`, honest `reason_summary` citing "legitimate"/"risk-lexicon filter", not an error); an unrelated clean candidate in the same population batch survives the drop (scoped, not over-broad). **Mutation-check shipped in the file** (`test_mutation_check_filter_is_load_bearing_not_vacuous`, using `monkeypatch` — safe, self-reverting): patches `f2._RISK_PHRASES` (the module-level name `_aggregate_disease_candidates` actually reads via `from src.eval.f2_grounding import _RISK_PHRASES`) to an empty tuple and confirms the SAME risky quote now SURVIVES as a shipped candidate — proving the sibling "dropped" assertion is genuinely exercising the filter, not passing for an unrelated reason. Independently confirmed developer reused the EXACT SAME `_RISK_PHRASES`/`_contains_any` primitives `filter_domain_candidates` already uses for `domain_candidates` (not a re-implemented/drifting copy) by direct read of `f2.py`'s imports and `_aggregate_disease_candidates`'s body. `pytest tests/test_f2_grounding.py -k Val011 -v` → **6/6 passed** (4 in the risk-lexicon-gate class + 2 in the provenance-boundary class, item 3 below).

**3. Provenance-enforcement deviation — developer's claim is TRUE on the live path, but NOT a standalone function-level invariant; flagged to critic, not blocking.** Independently scrutinized (not accepted at face value): `_aggregate_disease_candidates` constructs `AIPredictedDiseaseCandidate(source_id=chunk.get("chunk_id"), quote=quote)` — `chunk.get("chunk_id")` returns `None` (schema-legal, since `source_id` is `Optional[str]`) if the chunk dict lacks a `chunk_id` key. Direct-call confirmation (`f2._aggregate_disease_candidates([({"text": "...", "score": 0.6}, "불안장애", "불안")])`) DOES construct a candidate with `source_id=None` — no error raised. Separately, a chunk dict missing a `text` key produces an empty-string `quote` (`_extract_quote` returns `""`), which the schema's `min_length=1` constraint rejects with an uncaught `ValidationError` raised INSIDE `_aggregate_disease_candidates` itself (confirmed directly). **Why this does not reach the live path:** `f2.py::_run()`'s own `chunk_texts = {c["chunk_id"]: c["text"] for c in raw_chunks}` (`f2.py:864`) executes strictly BEFORE the population call (`f2.py:898`) and directly indexes both keys — it would already raise an uncaught `KeyError` and abort the entire F2 run for any `raw_chunks` entry missing `chunk_id`/`text`, before AI-disease population is ever reached. So a provenance-less candidate cannot ship today, but this is INCIDENTAL protection from an unrelated earlier line, not a designed invariant of the population code itself — the "no evidence, no candidate" discipline ADR-020 condition 2 requires is not actually enforced at `_aggregate_disease_candidates`'s own function boundary. Documented (not fixed — `src/` untouched per this gate's charter) in a new `TestVal011ProvenanceEnforcementBoundary` class (2 tests, both pass, both explicitly non-normative — they document current behavior, not desired behavior). **Verdict: schema-optional-but-code-enforced is adequate for shipping today (a candidate cannot actually ship without provenance on the live `_run()` path), but the enforcement is fragile — a future refactor of `_collect_chunk_disease_votes` or a differently-constructed votes list bypassing `retrieve_domain_chunks()`'s guaranteed shape could silently regress this. Flagged to critic for REV-017; not filed as a BUG and does not block this gate, per the brief's own instruction ("do not block on it unless a candidate can actually ship without provenance") — none does, today.**

**4. Labeling on the populated path — adequate, independently re-verified.** `grep -n "probability|확률|softmax|가능성(%)"` across `f2.py`/`ai_predicted_disease.py`/`retrieval.py` → all hits are docstring/description prose explicitly PROHIBITING those labels; no field or computed value uses them. MAX-not-sum aggregation confirmed by direct code read (`_aggregate_disease_candidates`'s `best.get(disease_name)`/`candidate.similarity_score > current.similarity_score` comparison, never a sum) and by an independently-constructed fixture (2 votes for the same disease, scores 0.4/0.9 -> ships 0.9 only, not 1.3) matching developer's own `test_max_not_sum_aggregation_across_chunks`. `is_diagnostic: Literal[False]` confirmed unchanged (schema diff: only `source_id`/`quote` fields added; `is_diagnostic`'s annotation untouched). Clamp verified: `f2._clamp_similarity_score(-0.05) == 0.0`, and a negative-score chunk run through the full `_aggregate_disease_candidates` path ships a candidate with `similarity_score=0.0` rather than raising — independently re-run, matches developer's own `test_negative_score_clamped_not_crashing`.

**5. #4 code-side summary counts — correct, independently spot-checked with a fresh (non-developer) fixture.** `f2._build_evidence_provenance_summary` on a 2-candidate/6-evidence-item fixture (2 case_card, 2 qa, 2 utterance, constructed by qa, not copied from developer's test) returns `{"rag_chunk_case_card": 2, "rag_chunk_qa": 2, "rag_chunk_other": 0, "utterance": 2}` — exact match. **`docs/ai/prompts/domain_inference/v2.system.md`: zero diff** (`git diff --stat` empty), confirming the certified prompt (ADR-018) is genuinely untouched. **`domain_candidates` shape: zero diff** on `src/schemas/domain_inference.py`, `src/agents/domain_inference.py`, `src/eval/f2_grounding.py` (`git diff --stat`, all empty) — the sibling entity and its risk-lexicon cascade are provably unmodified by this Track B work, independently confirming REV-016(c)'s "no ADR-018 invalidation" ruling holds structurally, not just by developer's own claim.

**6. Suite/lint/type/diff-scope.** `cd apps/ai-server && .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **807 passed, 0 failed** (798 baseline, independently re-run before any qa edit, + 9 new: 3 in `test_hpi_isolation.py`, 6 in `test_f2_grounding.py`). `ruff check` on both qa-touched files → all checks passed. `mypy` on both qa-touched files → the only 4 errors are the identical pre-existing `attr-defined`/`method-assign` false-positive pattern on `MagicMock(...).return_value =`-style adapter mocking, independently re-confirmed against a `git stash` baseline (byte-identical error set, same lines, before any qa edit) — no new mypy defect introduced. `git status --porcelain` confirms exactly 8 files changed: developer's 6 (`src/f2.py`, `src/rag/retrieval.py`, `src/schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/rag/test_retrieval.py`, `tests/test_ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/test_f2_pipeline.py`) + qa's 2 (`tests/test_hpi_isolation.py`, `tests/test_f2_grounding.py`), plus this `error.md` update — no `src/` file touched by qa, per this gate's constraint. Leakage-audit sanity (data-leakage-audit skill) on the 3 touched src files: no `gold`/`ground_truth`/`label` hits outside docstring prose prohibiting those labels; `score` hits are all retrieval cosine-similarity fields, not eval metrics; no test/val-split usage in model code.

**GATE: PASS.** VAL-011 closes as resolved — the risk-lexicon filter is genuinely code-enforced (reuses the exact ADR-014 primitives, mutation-checked non-vacuous), a risk-flagged candidate is DROPPED not clipped, a sole-risk-match reduces to a legitimate 0-candidate outcome, and the populated-path HPI red line is proven live (mutation-checked, non-vacuous) across both REV-013 §3 channels with a genuinely-populated container (not a synthetic marker) — REV-016(c) condition 2 is now satisfied. Population is APPROVED to ship from qa's gate. One non-blocking finding (item 3, provenance-enforcement fragility) flagged to critic for REV-017 — does not reopen VAL-011, does not block this gate, no BUG filed.

### Status update | 2026-07-09 | critic (REV-017, live evidence review) — REOPENED, blocking

**Status:** reopened — blocking. Supersedes qa's "closes as resolved" verdict above for the specific gap this update documents; qa's own verification (mutation-tested, non-vacuous fixture) is not disputed — the fixture's *scope* was too narrow to catch this failure mode.

**Finding.** `f2._aggregate_disease_candidates` (`f2.py:450-457`) checks `_contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES)` where `quote` is `_extract_quote`'s **±40-character window centered on the matched symptom keyword** (`f2.py:358-376`) — not the full `chunk.get("text")`. ADR-020 condition 1's own binding text requires dropping "any candidate whose **sole matched chunk** is risk-lexicon-flagged" — a chunk-level guarantee the shipped code does not provide; it provides only a quote-window-level check.

**Live reproduction (independently derived from raw artifacts, not from `result.md`'s prose), twice, same persona:**
- `experiments/EXP-009/runs/VP-003/run2/VP-003/VP-003_20260709_182025_domain_inference.json` (k=3, the certified default): `ai_predicted_disease.candidates` ships **"우울 삽화(우울증)"** and **"소아·청소년 우울증"**, both `source_id: "case_card:664"` — the SAME run's `domain_candidates.whitelist_audit.verdicts` independently rejects `case_card:664` as `rejected_risk_lexicon` (`reason: "...(자해)..."`). `case_card:664`'s full `chunk_texts` entry reads "...자해를 하려는 생각이 가끔 들기도 하며..."; the shipped disease-candidate quote ("...느끼는 경우가 있다. 내담자는 자신을 쓸모없다고...") starts immediately after that clause, outside the 40-char window.
- `experiments/EXP-010/runs/k5/VP-003/run2/VP-003/VP-003_20260709_182833_domain_inference.json` (k=5, same VP-003/run2 input): identical `case_card:664` pattern reproduces verbatim, **plus** `"월경전 불쾌장애"`/`"급성 스트레스장애"` sourced from `case_card:563` (full text: "...자살 생각을 표현하며...", same-run `domain_candidates` whitelist independently rejects it for `"...(자살)..."`), quote starting after the flagged clause.

**Why the shipped test missed it:** `tests/test_f2_grounding.py::TestVal011AiPredictedDiseasePopulationRiskLexiconGate` (`:561-577`) uses a single-sentence fixture (~35 chars) where the matched term is necessarily within the 40-char window of the risk phrase — structurally unable to exercise the long-chunk/distant-term case `case_card:664`/`case_card:563` (several sentences each) demonstrate live.

**Not a wholesale filter failure** — confirmed the filter correctly drops the majority of votes this batch (EXP-009's own per-run dropped-vote counts match `reason_summary` exactly in all 8 runs; VP-004/run2's own `case_card:664` vote was correctly dropped that specific run, where the matched term evidently fell closer to the risk phrase). The gap is a specific, characterized, fixable blind spot tied to extraction-window placement, not an inoperative filter.

**Resolution:** check `_contains_any` against the full `chunk.get("text")` (not only the extracted `quote`) at `f2.py:455`, or check both; add a long-chunk/distant-term regression fixture reproducing `case_card:664`'s actual shape to `TestVal011AiPredictedDiseasePopulationRiskLexiconGate`; qa re-gates with a mutation check on the new fixture; a clean re-verification batch must run before "shippable"/"resolved" wording is reused for Track B's populated path.

**Full reasoning, artifact citations, and the shippability ruling this reopening is part of:** REV-017 (`discussion.md`), §1/Issue #1.

**Linked:** REV-017 (`discussion.md`), EXP-009, EXP-010 (`result.md`), ADR-020, PLAN-2026-W28-K.

**Status:** resolved — full-chunk risk-lexicon check independently reproduced closing the live leak, confirmed load-bearing by mutation, confirmed non-over-blocking. **GATE: PASS** for the code-fix dimension. "Shippable"/"certified" wording for Track B's populated path still requires critic re-review (REV-018, out of this gate's charter) before use — this entry closes VAL-011's own reopened defect, it does not itself relicense shippability language.

**0. Fix located, matches the resolution REV-017 specified.** `apps/ai-server/src/f2.py:465`: `if _contains_any(chunk_text, _RISK_PHRASES) or _contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES):` — checks the **full source chunk text** (`chunk_text = chunk.get("text") or ""`, `f2.py:445`) in addition to the ±40-char extracted `quote`, exactly the resolution REV-017 §"What would resolve it" specified (line offset ~455→465 is comment-only drift, same function/location REV-017 cited).

This closes REV-017's own stated re-verification bar ("Re-run `TestVal011AiPredictedDiseasePopulationRiskLexiconGate` with a long-chunk, distant-term fixture reproducing `case_card:664`'s actual shape") using the real artifact data, not only the shipped synthetic regression fixture.

**3. Mutation check — load-bearing, not vacuous.** Reverted `_aggregate_disease_candidates`'s check in-memory (via `exec` on a source-patched copy of the function, no file touched) from `_contains_any(chunk_text, _RISK_PHRASES) or _contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES)` back to quote-only (`_contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES)` alone — the exact pre-fix REV-017-documented state), and re-ran the same three votes (case_card:664, case_card:563, my synthetic fixture):

AFTER  mutation (quote-only, pre-fix): candidates: [('우울 삽화(우울증)', 'case_card:664'),

The mutation reproduces the exact live leak REV-017 found (same two disease names, same two `source_id`s) — confirming the full-chunk check is genuinely load-bearing and not incidental to some other code path. (The synthetic MYTEST1 vote does not reappear under mutation for an unrelated, expected reason — MAX-not-sum aggregation: it shares a disease name with the higher-scoring `case_card:664` vote (0.9 vs 0.7) and is shadowed, not filtered; confirmed by disease-name/score inspection, not assumed.)

**4. No over-blocking regression.** `TestRev017Finding1FullChunkRiskLexiconGate::test_long_chunk_no_risk_phrase_anywhere_still_ships_no_over_blocking` (developer's shipped fixture, independently re-run) — a benign, same-length/shape long multi-sentence chunk with **no** risk phrase anywhere ships a candidate normally (`n_dropped == 0`, 1 candidate, correct `source_id`). All 4 pre-existing `TestVal011AiPredictedDiseasePopulationRiskLexiconGate` tests (short-fixture, original VAL-011 scope) and all 12 `tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` tests (9 pre-existing Wave-3 + 3 new populated-path) pass unmodified: `pytest tests/test_f2_grounding.py -k "Val011 or Rev017" -v` → **11/11 passed**; `pytest tests/test_hpi_isolation.py -v` → **12/12 passed**. Diffed `tests/test_hpi_isolation.py` directly — the 9 pre-existing tests are byte-for-byte unmodified (pure append at EOF); no existing assertion was weakened anywhere in the 8-file diff (`git diff | grep -c '^-[^-]'` on `test_f2_pipeline.py`/`test_ai_predicted_disease.py` → 0; `test_retrieval.py` → 1, an import-line addition, not an assertion change).

Ruff/mypy.** `ruff check src/ tests/` → all checks passed. `mypy src/f2.py src/schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py src/rag/retrieval.py` → 2 pre-existing errors in `retrieval.py` (import-untyped on `contracts.chat`; a `Sequence[Row]`-vs-`list[Any]` return-type mismatch in the unrelated, untouched `_topk` function) — confirmed pre-existing via `git stash`/re-run against the unmodified HEAD (identical 2 errors, same lines, before this diff). `f2.py`/`ai_predicted_disease.py`: no issues. `mypy tests/test_f2_grounding.py tests/test_hpi_isolation.py tests/test_f2_pipeline.py tests/test_ai_predicted_disease.py tests/rag/test_retrieval.py` → 4 errors, all in `test_hpi_isolation.py:212,227-229`, the identical pre-existing `attr-defined`/`method-assign` `MagicMock(...).return_value =` false-positive pattern already baselined in this project's history (BUG-016/017/019) — confirmed these exact lines sit in the pre-existing `_wire_echo` helper (lines 200-228, before this diff's insertion point at line 328), not new code.

**7. Diff-scope — deviates from the HANDOFF's stated claim; flagged, not blocking.** HANDOFF states "Only `src/f2.py` + `tests/test_f2_grounding.py` touched." Actual: `git status --porcelain` shows **8 files** modified (`src/f2.py`, `src/rag/retrieval.py`, `src/schemas/ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/rag/test_retrieval.py`, `tests/test_ai_predicted_disease.py`, `tests/test_f2_grounding.py`, `tests/test_f2_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_hpi_isolation.py`). This diff is not an incremental patch on an already-committed population feature; it is the full ADR-020 path1 feature (previously reviewed live via EXP-009/EXP-010/REV-017 on a different lineage) landing in this branch's working tree in one sitting, with the REV-017 fix authored directly into it rather than as a follow-up edit. Verified this is not scope creep: every non-`f2.py`/non-`test_f2_grounding.py` file's diff is additive-only (0-1 non-context removed lines each, checked above) and implements exactly the ADR-020/REV-016-approved population mechanics (schema fields, `match_diseases_for_chunk_text`, their own test coverage) — nothing outside Track B's already-approved scope. The REV-017-specific fix itself (`f2.py:465` + the new `TestRev017Finding1FullChunkRiskLexiconGate` class in `test_f2_grounding.py`) is exactly where the HANDOFF said it would be. Filed as a factual correction to the HANDOFF's scope claim, not a defect.

**Verdict:** VAL-011 closes as resolved. The `_contains_any(chunk_text, _RISK_PHRASES) or _contains_any(quote, _RISK_PHRASES)` full-chunk check genuinely closes the REV-017 Finding 1 gap — independently reproduced (synthetic fixture + real EXP-009 artifact replay of both live leak instances), mutation-confirmed load-bearing (reverting to quote-only reproduces the exact leak), and confirmed non-over-blocking (benign long chunks still ship, all pre-existing VAL-011/HPI-isolation tests pass unmodified). Suite green at 812/812, ruff clean, mypy clean modulo pre-existing baseline noise. **This does not itself license "shippable"/"certified" wording for Track B's populated path** — REV-017's Issue #2 (secondary-audit methodological caveat) and Issue #3 (tooling-constraint note) are unresolved by this gate (out of charter) and REV-017's overall ruling requires a critic re-review (REV-018) of a clean re-verification batch before shippability language is reused, per REV-017's own resolution table item 1 ("experiment-tracker re-runs a clean VP-003(+VP-001) populated-path n=2 batch; critic re-reviews before any 'shippable' wording is used").

### Critic re-review (REV-018, final closure — "shippable" wording licensed) | 2026-07-09 | critic

**Status:** resolved, final. No further re-verification required as a precondition for shippable wording.

Independently re-verified against EXP-011's raw artifacts (not accepted from qa's or the tracker's prose): both leak instances REV-017 documented (`case_card:664`, `case_card:563`) are retrieved again this batch — `case_card:664` twice, independently, across two different VP/run pairs (`VP-003/run2`, `VP-004/run2`) — and in every occurrence are correctly excluded from the shipped `ai_predicted_disease.candidates` list. The new chunk-level audit (0/33) was independently re-derived by reading every `chunk_texts` entry tied to every shipped candidate across all 8 EXP-011 runs, not accepted from the tracker's count. No over-blocking regression found on real batch data (benign personas ship legitimate, risk-clean candidate sets). HPI isolation independently re-confirmed at the artifact level on genuinely-populated (non-synthetic) disease names.

**"Shippable" wording is now licensed for Track B's populated path**, subject to five mandatory caveats restated in REV-018 §2: (1) two-hop-proxy disclosure, (2) VAL-010-open disclosure, (3) single-batch-evidence disclosure, (4) provenance-enforcement-fragility follow-up note, (5) no-diagnosis-language (`is_diagnostic: False`). ADR-018's certification is confirmed unaffected. VAL-010 stays open and does not block (REV-012 §5's established ruling, not re-litigated). Standing conditions (secondary taxonomy audit + new chunk-level audit + queries audit + mode-honesty reporting) remain mandatory on every future populated-path batch — this closure does not suspend them.

**Full reasoning, artifact citations, and the shippability ruling:** REV-018 (`discussion.md`), full entry.

**Linked:** REV-018 (`discussion.md`), EXP-011 (`result.md`), ADR-018, ADR-020, VAL-010 (open, unaffected).
