## [BUG-028] EXP-015 VP-003 cert run: DialogueAgent's corrected retry-hint fires but fails to diversify output — live repetition-guard trip truncates F1 at turn 9/10 | 2026-07-11 | qa

**Severity:** major
**Component:** `apps/ai-server/src/agents/dialogue.py:211-250` (`is_repeated`/retry-with-stronger-hint, code unchanged since the BUG-025 fix at `4be9818` and confirmed firing correctly this run); `apps/ai-server/src/f1.py:1655-1665` (session-level exact-repeat guard, unmodified, working as designed — not the defect)
**Linked:** REV-025 (Finding, `discussion.md`, this bug's originating investigation, Resolution item 2), BUG-025 (repetition-guard lineage — its own specific fix verified unaffected/unimplicated, see Causal assessment below), BUG-004 (`DialogueAgent repetition loop`, original lineage entry, still open), EXP-015 (`result.md`, source run — correction note below), ADR-025 (post-fix full re-run precedent, cited for framing only)
**Status:** open — n=1 live observation under stochastic adaptive-`PatientLLM` conditions; root cause not fully disambiguated

### Reproduction

Not independently re-run this gate (brief constraint: no vendor/LLM calls) — reconstructed from the existing EXP-015 cert-batch artifacts, every line cited to source.

| Step | Action |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | Source artifacts (already on disk, no new run performed): `experiments/EXP-015/runs/cert/VP-003/f1_stdout.log` (full read) and `docs/ai/simulation_results/VP-003/VP-003_20260711_150000_conversation.json` (full read) — the live F1→F2 Gate-0 certification run for VP-003, `feat/f1f2-program-w29` @ `325daa4`, dialogue v3 pin `d8870e5d...4325` (post-BUG-025-fix). |
| 2 | Confirm `f1.py`'s actual `max_turns` for this run: `apps/ai-server/src/f1.py:2276` — CLI default `--max-turns` is **10**, not 12; `experiments/EXP-015/config.yaml`'s `runs[].steps[].args` for the F1 stage passes only `--persona`/`--out`, no `--max-turns` override. **Correction to this investigation's own dispatching brief, which cited "of max 12"** — the actual configured cap this run was 10. |
| 3 | Turn-by-turn `agent_response` read directly from the conversation JSON: turn 6 = "...최근에 정신과 치료나 진단을 받으신 적이..." (targeted_slot=`personal_social_history`); turn 7 = "...혹시 과거에 정신과 치료나 진단을 받으신 적이 있는지..." (targeted_slot=`past_psychiatric_history`) — differs from turn 6 by one word (최근에→과거에), NOT byte-identical, so neither `dialogue.py`'s internal `is_repeated` nor `f1.py`'s outer guard trips at turn 7. |
| 4 | Turn 8 `agent_response` is **byte-identical** to turn 7's (same targeted_slot, `past_psychiatric_history`, repeated) → `f1.py:1656`'s `agent_response == prev_agent_response` fires: `f1_stdout.log:165` `"Agent repeating (count=1)"`, `result.errors.append("Agent repetition at turn 8 (count=1)")` (confirmed in the persisted `errors` array, `conversation.json:674`). |
| 5 | Turn 9 `agent_response` is **byte-identical** to turn 8's again (this time targeted_slot=`medical_history` per the round-robin computation, but the actual returned text is unchanged, still asking about psychiatric history — the hint's own topic was not honored) → guard fires again: `f1_stdout.log:183-184` `"Agent repeating (count=2)"` → `"Agent repeated 2+ times — stopping"`. Session ends at turn 9 (of the 10-turn cap), one turn short of exhausting its own configured budget naturally. |
| 6 | The patient's own utterance (`patient_message`) is byte-identical across turns 7, 8, and 9 verbatim (turn 6's is near-identical, one leading `"..."` token difference) — the adaptive `PatientLLM` (served via a separate `api.friendli.ai` backend, confirmed by the interleaved `httpx` POSTs in `f1_stdout.log`) appears to have stalled on the same non-answer for 3-4 consecutive turns. |
| 7 | `dialogue.py`'s own internal repeat-detector (`is_repeated`, compares the fresh LLM draft against every prior assistant turn, not just the immediately-preceding one) fired independently 5 times this session — `f1_stdout.log:86,111,128,158,176` (`"DialogueAgent repeated — retrying with stronger hint"`), covering turns 4, 5, 6, 8, and 9. At turns 4/5/6 the retry successfully produced a genuinely different final response (confirmed: turn 4/5/6 `agent_response` values are all pairwise distinct). At turns 8 and 9 the retry fired but the **final, post-retry** response was still byte-identical to the immediately-preceding turn both times — i.e. the corrected retry-hint code path (`missing_questionable_slots`, the exact BUG-025 fix) engaged and presumably supplied a topically-relevant, non-empty hint, and the underlying LLM still returned the earlier text verbatim. |

**Expected:** per BUG-025's own fix and REV-025's Gate-0 confirmation that dialogue v3's prompt/hint code is sound, a retry firing with a correct, non-empty, topically-relevant missing-slot hint should produce a genuinely different assistant response, or — failing that — the exact-repeat guard should be the *only* line of defense and its trip should not coincide with the mandatory SI screen being left unasked (BUG-025's own red line).
**Actual:** the retry mechanism fired correctly (confirmed via the WARNING logs) but did not diversify the output on 2 of 5 firings this session (turns 8 and 9), both immediately following 3-4 consecutive turns of byte-identical patient input — causing the outer exact-repeat guard to trip twice and truncate the session at turn 9/10. **Partial mitigation, not a clean pass:** unlike BUG-025's SM-06 case, the mandatory SI/`risk_assessment` screen was **already asked and grounded** by turn 6 (`probe_events` shows `risk_assessment_written: true` at turns 3 and 6; `final_slots` includes a populated `risk_assessment` entry) — unaffected by the truncation. But truncation still left **2 of 5 `ESSENTIAL_SLOT_KEYS`** unfilled (`mental_status_exam`, `clinical_assessment` — neither ever collected) and **4 of 8 `QUESTIONABLE_SLOT_KEYS`** unfilled (`past_psychiatric_history`, `medical_history`, `personal_social_history`, `substance_use_history`) — confirmed by direct diff of `final_slots` (4 entries: `chief_complaint`, `history_of_present_illness`, `risk_assessment`, `family_history`) against `apps/ai-server/src/agents/clinical_slot.py:41-47`/`apps/ai-server/src/grounding.py:43-52`. The natural slot-completion termination gate (`f1.py:1645-1653`, `other_missing` empty AND `risk_grounded`) had **not** yet been met (4 of the 7 non-risk questionable slots were still missing) — the repetition guard, not slot completion, is what ended this session.

**Correction to EXP-015's own record (per this bug's brief, item warranting an error.md-side note; `result.md` is tracker territory, not edited here):** `result.md` EXP-015's "BUG-021 check" section states VP-003's F1 run logged "2 `InputNormalizer response parse failed` WARNINGs (`f1_stdout.log:34,94`)" and that the run's own `Errors: 2` summary line is "consistent with this." Independently re-read: `f1_stdout.log` has **3** such WARNINGs (lines 34, 58, 94 — line 58 has a distinct signature, a raw JSON-decode `Unterminated string` failure, not `BUG-020`'s specific `NormalizationChange` Pydantic-validation-error signature), and `Errors: 2` is **not** attributable to `InputNormalizerAgent` at all — `result.errors` (`f1.py:212`, the field the summary line counts) is only ever appended to by the exact-repeat guard (`f1.py:1658`), which is this bug's own mechanism, firing at turns 8 and 9. This matches REV-025's independent finding verbatim (`discussion.md`, REV-025 Issues #1/#2) — recorded here as the qa-side corroboration and correction note this bug's filing brief calls for.

**Causal assessment — new mechanism, not a BUG-025 recurrence:** BUG-025's own root cause (fixed at `4be9818`, confirmed unchanged/still-landed this run via the `d8870e5d...` prompt pin and a direct read of `dialogue.py:235`'s `missing_questionable_slots` call) was a **code/prompt defect**: the retry hint either named the wrong slots or none at all, or always-on prompt content crowded out the instruction. Here, the retry-hint code path is confirmed unmodified and firing on schedule (5/5 internal triggers logged, each followed by a real retry HTTP call, none raising) — the defect, if there is a single stable one, is not in which slots the hint names. What's new and undiagnosed is that the underlying LLM (`solar-pro3`) reproduced its own immediately-prior output verbatim twice in a row, in a request that explicitly instructed otherwise and named specific missing slots, immediately downstream of 3-4 turns of byte-identical patient input. Two live candidate explanations, **not distinguishable from this log alone** (matching REV-025's own hedge): (a) an LLM instruction-following failure under repetitive/low-information context — the same higher-level class BUG-004 (still open) named as the original risk this whole guard exists to bound; (b) a patient-simulator-side stall (the `friendli.ai`-served `PatientLLM` producing the same non-answer 3-4 times) that starves `DialogueAgent` of any new information to react to, making a genuinely novel question harder to justify even with the hint present. Filed as a **new** entry rather than an addendum to BUG-025 because BUG-025's specific, already-fixed defect is demonstrably not implicated (its fix code is present, correct, and executing) — this is the guard's *backstop* function being exercised again, under a different upstream trigger, exactly the scenario REV-025 flagged as untested by the scripted SM matrix (SM-01..08b passed clean 4/4 post-fix, per BUG-025's own r2 verification, but that matrix runs fixed, scripted patient turns — it cannot exercise an adaptive-`PatientLLM` stall).

**Contrast (VP-001, same batch, brief item (e)):** `experiments/EXP-015/runs/cert/VP-001/f1_stdout.log` — full 10/10 turns completed, `Errors: 0`, zero `"DialogueAgent repeated"` or `"Agent repeat"` log lines anywhere in the file. VP-001's F1 result line: `Turns: 10 | ... | Errors: 0`. Confirms the mechanism is VP-003-specific this batch, not a general session-ending defect on every run — consistent with REV-025's own framing.

**Sample-size caveat (disclosed, not hidden):** n=1 live observation on a stochastic adaptive-`PatientLLM` pairing. This is the *first* time this repetition-guard mechanism class has been observed to trip under live, adaptive-patient conditions (as opposed to the fixed, scripted SM-01..08b matrix, which is clean post-`4be9818`) — informative but not yet a measured recurrence rate.

**Minimal repro:** not possible offline without a vendor/LLM call (out of this gate's constraints) — the trigger condition (an adaptive `PatientLLM` producing 3-4 consecutive near-identical turns, followed by `DialogueAgent`'s own LLM failing to diversify twice despite a corrected hint) is not deterministically reproducible from static fixtures. Recommend developer/qa add a **mocked** regression test once a candidate code-level intervention is proposed (e.g. assert the retry escalates further — a second retry attempt, or a hard-coded fallback question — rather than accepting a still-identical post-retry response as final); until then, this entry's evidence is the live artifacts cited above: `experiments/EXP-015/runs/cert/VP-003/f1_stdout.log`, `docs/ai/simulation_results/VP-003/VP-003_20260711_150000_conversation.json`.

**Recommendation for orchestrator (W7b disposition):** do not block W7b on this n=1 finding — the guard is functioning as a correct safety backstop (bounded a stalled session rather than looping indefinitely) and the mandatory SI screen was not compromised in this instance. But do not silently absorb it into a "legitimate stochastic outcome" framing either (this project's own established discipline, per REV-025's Gate-0 verdict and ADR-025's bisection-before-clean-claim precedent). Recommended path: **proceed with a pre-registered truncation-rate disclosure** — W7b's SC-1/SC-2/SC-3 baseline collection should track and disclose the rate of sessions ending via the exact-repeat guard before natural slot-completion, alongside the existing per-cell reporting, rather than either (i) blocking W7b entirely on a single stochastic n=1 data point with no identified code defect, or (ii) proceeding without naming this as a known, live, unresolved truncation mode. If the disclosed rate at n=2/VP crosses a materially non-trivial threshold, escalate to a developer investigation at that point (this entry stays open pending that signal).

**Target:** `PLAN-2026-W28-Q` W6-end joint orchestrator+critic transcription-verification gate (`REV-023` ruling 2, Issue 5, blocking-scoped; `REV-026` ruling 4/5); `.claude/hooks/archive_gate.py` Phase 2 arming.
**Issue:** two blocking gaps found on independent re-check of the ratified 7-item + 3-appendix checklist: (1) remedy i ("per-run RAG face-validity check," `CVR-001`/plan §8a) survives only as a bare name+owner+wave — its operational content (candidate-list differentiation across VPs; age/sex/classification plausibility of each shipped disease name) was authored once in `discussion.md` `CVR-001`'s Recommendations and never transcribed to any surviving doc; (2) `REV-026` ruling 5's own named pre-arming condition — a qa symlink audit of `_archive`/`docs/ai/personas` — has not been executed or recorded as PASS anywhere; `docs/ai/workflow_results_f1f2.md`'s own W6 entry states it "remains routed to qa, not executed."
**Why blocking:** `archive_gate.py` Phase 2 arming and `VER-00X` execution are gated on the W6-end transcription-verification checklist being satisfied (`REV-023` ruling 2's binding condition, restated in `docs/ai/validation_plan_f1f2_continuous.md` §7). Arming before either gap closes would (1) let a pre-registered W8 clinical check survive to the blind wave in name only, forcing the blind CV to invent the check's criteria after seeing live RAG output — the exact "criterion reworded after seeing results" failure this project's pre-registration discipline exists to prevent — and (2) leave a named, still-open security/integrity pre-arming condition from a prior review unresolved at the moment the mechanism it gates activates.
**Linked review:** REV-027
